[index (14).html](https://github.com/user-attachments/files/26844998/index.14.html)
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="en">
<head>
<meta charset="UTF-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width,initial-scale=1.0">
<title>C&W NSW Sales Intelligence</title>
<link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Barlow:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=Barlow+Condensed:wght@400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
<link rel="stylesheet" href="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.9.4/leaflet.min.css"/>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/leaflet/1.9.4/leaflet.min.js"></script>
<script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/xlsx/0.18.5/xlsx.full.min.js"></script>
<style>
:root {
  --red:#E1251B; --red-d:#B01E15; --red-pale:#FDE8E7; --red-mid:rgba(225,37,27,.1);
  --ink:#231F20; --dark:#3A3A3A; --mid:#6D6E71; --light:#D1D3D4; --pale:#F5F5F5;
  --white:#FFFFFF; --border:#E0E0E0;
  --green:#2E7D32; --green-pale:rgba(46,125,50,.1);
  --amber:#B8860B; --amber-pale:rgba(184,134,11,.12);
  --blue:#1565C0; --blue-pale:rgba(21,101,192,.1);
  --metro:#1565C0; --metro-pale:rgba(21,101,192,.08);
  --land:#2E7D32; --land-pale:rgba(46,125,50,.08);
  --prem:#E65100; --prem-pale:rgba(230,81,0,.08);
}
*{box-sizing:border-box;margin:0;padding:0}
body{font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;background:var(--pale);color:var(--ink);font-size:14px;min-height:100vh;overflow:hidden}
/* HEADER */
header{background:var(--ink);color:var(--white);padding:0 1.4rem;height:52px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:200;border-bottom:3px solid var(--red);flex-shrink:0}
.logo{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.85rem}
.logo-icon{width:28px;height:28px;flex-shrink:0}
.logo-text .main{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase;line-height:1}
.logo-text .sub{font-size:.56rem;color:var(--light);letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:1px}
.hdr-actions{display:flex;gap:.38rem;align-items:center}
/* BUTTONS */
.btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.28rem;padding:.34rem .78rem;border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.74rem;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:1.5px solid transparent;transition:all .13s;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;white-space:nowrap;line-height:1.2}
.btn-red{background:var(--red);color:var(--white);border-color:var(--red)}.btn-red:hover{background:var(--red-d)}
.btn-ghost{background:transparent;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.2)}.btn-ghost:hover{color:var(--white);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.5)}
.btn-outline{background:transparent;color:var(--ink);border-color:var(--border)}.btn-outline:hover{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red)}
.btn-green{background:var(--green);color:var(--white);border-color:var(--green)}.btn-green:hover{background:#1b5e20}
.btn-blue{background:var(--blue);color:var(--white)}.btn-blue:hover{background:#0d47a1}
.btn-sm{padding:.24rem .55rem;font-size:.68rem}
/* LAYOUT */
.app{display:flex;height:calc(100vh - 52px);overflow:hidden}
nav{width:185px;min-width:185px;background:var(--ink);display:flex;flex-direction:column;padding:.45rem 0;overflow-y:auto;flex-shrink:0}
.nav-group-label{padding:.6rem .9rem .18rem;font-size:.54rem;color:#555;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.15em;font-weight:600}
.nav-item{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.45rem;padding:.44rem .88rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,.48);cursor:pointer;font-size:.76rem;font-weight:500;border-left:2px solid transparent;transition:all .11s;user-select:none}
.nav-item:hover{color:rgba(255,255,255,.85);background:rgba(255,255,255,.04)}
.nav-item.active{color:var(--red);border-left-color:var(--red);background:rgba(225,37,27,.07)}
.nav-item svg{width:12px;height:12px;flex-shrink:0;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2}
.nav-badge{background:var(--red);color:var(--white);border-radius:10px;padding:.06rem .34rem;font-size:.56rem;font-weight:700;margin-left:auto;line-height:1.4}
.nav-badge.blue{background:var(--blue)}.nav-badge.amber{background:var(--amber)}.nav-badge.green{background:var(--green)}
.main{flex:1;overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;min-width:0}
/* STATS BAR */
.stats-bar{background:var(--white);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding:.55rem 1.3rem;display:flex;gap:1.1rem;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;overflow-x:auto}
.stat{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.06rem;flex-shrink:0}
.stat-lbl{font-size:.54rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mid)}
.stat-val{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.18rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--ink);line-height:1}
.stat-val.red{color:var(--red)}.stat-val.green{color:var(--green)}.stat-val.amber{color:var(--amber)}.stat-val.blue{color:var(--blue)}
.sdiv{width:1px;background:var(--border);height:24px;flex-shrink:0}
/* DB SELECTOR */
.db-selector{background:var(--ink);padding:.48rem 1.3rem;display:flex;gap:.35rem;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.06)}
.db-btn{padding:.3rem .9rem;border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.15);border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;color:rgba(255,255,255,.45);background:transparent;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;transition:all .12s}
.db-btn:hover{color:var(--white);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.35)}
.db-btn.active{color:var(--white)!important}
.db-btn.all.active{background:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)}
.db-btn.metro.active{background:var(--metro);border-color:var(--metro)}
.db-btn.land.active{background:var(--land);border-color:var(--land)}
.db-btn.prem.active{background:var(--prem);border-color:var(--prem)}
.db-rec-count{margin-left:auto;font-size:.69rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,.35);font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif}
/* VIEWS */
.view{display:none;flex:1;flex-direction:column;overflow:hidden;min-height:0}
.view.active{display:flex}
/* TOOLBAR */
.toolbar{padding:.55rem 1.3rem;display:flex;gap:.45rem;align-items:center;background:var(--white);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);flex-wrap:wrap;flex-shrink:0}
.toolbar-title{font-weight:600;font-size:.8rem;white-space:nowrap}
.tb-r{margin-left:auto;display:flex;gap:.38rem;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0}
.search-wrap{position:relative;flex:1;min-width:140px;max-width:255px}
.search-wrap input{width:100%;padding:.34rem .55rem .34rem 1.75rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.76rem;background:var(--pale)}
.search-wrap input:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--red)}
.search-icon{position:absolute;left:.48rem;top:50%;transform:translateY(-50%);color:var(--mid);width:11px;height:11px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;pointer-events:none}
select.filt{padding:.34rem .42rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.74rem;background:var(--pale);cursor:pointer;color:var(--ink)}
select.filt:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--red)}
.cnt{font-size:.68rem;color:var(--mid);white-space:nowrap}
/* TABLE */
.table-wrap{flex:1;overflow:auto;min-height:0}
table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.77rem}
thead th{background:var(--ink);color:var(--white);padding:.48rem .62rem;text-align:left;font-size:.59rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;white-space:nowrap;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:10}
thead th:first-child{border-left:3px solid var(--red)}
tbody tr{border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);cursor:pointer;transition:background .09s}
tbody tr:hover{background:var(--red-pale)}
tbody tr:nth-child(even){background:#fafafa}
tbody tr:nth-child(even):hover{background:var(--red-pale)}
td{padding:.52rem .62rem;vertical-align:middle}
.t-addr{font-weight:600;max-width:190px}
.t-addr .sub{font-size:.67rem;color:var(--mid);font-weight:400;margin-top:1px}
.mono{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.85rem;font-weight:600}
.yv{color:var(--red);font-weight:700}
/* BADGES */
.badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:.13rem .38rem;border-radius:1px;font-size:.57rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
.b-sold{background:var(--green-pale);color:var(--green)}
.b-active{background:var(--red-mid);color:var(--red)}
.b-withdrawn{background:rgba(109,110,113,.1);color:var(--mid)}
.b-stale{background:var(--amber-pale);color:var(--amber)}
.db-tag{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:.12rem .38rem;border-radius:1px;font-size:.56rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.03em}
.db-tag.metro{background:var(--metro-pale);color:var(--metro)}
.db-tag.land{background:var(--land-pale);color:var(--land)}
.db-tag.prem{background:var(--prem-pale);color:var(--prem)}
.guide-tag{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:.22rem;background:var(--amber-pale);border:1px solid rgba(184,134,11,.2);border-radius:2px;padding:.12rem .4rem;font-size:.64rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--amber)}
/* EMPTY STATE */
.empty-state{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:3rem;color:var(--mid);text-align:center;gap:.4rem}
.empty-state .icon{font-size:2.2rem}
.empty-state strong{font-size:.88rem;color:var(--dark)}
.empty-state p{font-size:.78rem}
/* CAMPAIGN TRACKER */
.camp-kpis{display:flex;gap:.68rem;padding:.78rem 1.3rem;background:var(--ink);flex-shrink:0;flex-wrap:wrap}
.ckpi{background:rgba(255,255,255,.06);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.09);border-radius:2px;padding:.52rem .85rem;min-width:105px}
.ckpi-l{font-size:.54rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:rgba(255,255,255,.5);margin-bottom:.08rem}
.ckpi-v{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.4rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--white);line-height:1}
.ckpi-v.red{color:#FF6B6B}.ckpi-v.amber{color:#FFC107}.ckpi-v.green{color:#81C784}
.dpill{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:.15rem .48rem;border-radius:20px;font-size:.68rem;font-weight:700;font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;min-width:42px}
.dp-today{background:var(--red);color:var(--white)}.dp-soon{background:var(--amber);color:var(--white)}
.dp-active{background:var(--green-pale);color:var(--green)}.dp-past{background:var(--pale);color:var(--mid)}
.dp-settle{background:var(--blue-pale);color:var(--blue)}
.spill{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:.13rem .42rem;border-radius:1px;font-size:.59rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em}
.sp-today{background:var(--red);color:var(--white)}.sp-week{background:var(--amber);color:var(--white)}
.sp-active{background:var(--green-pale);color:var(--green)}.sp-past{background:var(--pale);color:var(--mid)}
.sp-settle{background:var(--blue-pale);color:var(--blue)}
.msb{background:var(--green);color:var(--white);border:none;border-radius:2px;padding:.22rem .55rem;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.67rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;cursor:pointer}
.msb:hover{background:#1b5e20}
/* SETTLEMENT */
.settle-hdr{background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--blue) 0%,#0d47a1 100%);color:var(--white);padding:.78rem 1.3rem;flex-shrink:0}
.settle-hdr h2{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.9rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em}
.skpi-row{display:flex;gap:.9rem;margin-top:.42rem}
.skpi-v{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.25rem;font-weight:700}
.skpi-l{font-size:.56rem;opacity:.7;letter-spacing:.08em;text-transform:uppercase}
/* BENCHMARK */
.bench-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.85rem}
.bench-controls{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:.85rem;display:flex;gap:.62rem;align-items:flex-end;flex-wrap:wrap}
.bench-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:.85rem}
.bench-card{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:.9rem;border-top:3px solid var(--red)}
.bench-card h3{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.64rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.72rem;font-weight:700}
.bsr{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;padding:.3rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--pale);font-size:.78rem}
.bsr:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.bsl{color:var(--mid)}
.bsv{font-weight:600;font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.86rem}
.bsv.red{color:var(--red)}
.br-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.48rem;margin-bottom:.35rem}
.br-lbl{font-size:.68rem;color:var(--mid);min-width:86px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.br-track{flex:1;background:var(--pale);border-radius:1px;height:5px;overflow:hidden}
.br-fill{height:100%;background:var(--red);border-radius:1px}
.br-val{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.72rem;color:var(--ink);font-weight:600;min-width:58px;text-align:right}
.ctable{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.75rem}
.ctable th{background:var(--pale);padding:.36rem .56rem;text-align:left;font-size:.59rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;color:var(--mid);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.ctable td{padding:.42rem .56rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.ctable tr:hover td{background:var(--red-pale);cursor:pointer}
/* AGENTS */
.league-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem}
.league-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:.85rem;margin-bottom:.85rem}
.league-card{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:.9rem;border-top:3px solid var(--red)}
.league-card h3{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.64rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.72rem;font-weight:700}
.ag-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.62rem;padding:.4rem 0;border-bottom:1px solid var(--pale)}
.ag-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.ag-rank{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.86rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--mid);min-width:20px}
.ag-rank.gold{color:#B8860B}.ag-rank.silver{color:#888}.ag-rank.bronze{color:#CD7F32}
.ag-name{flex:1;font-size:.78rem;font-weight:500;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.ag-vol{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.78rem;font-weight:600;min-width:66px;text-align:right}
/* MAP */
.map-wrap{flex:1;position:relative}
#mapEl{width:100%;height:100%}
/* ANALYTICS */
.analytics-wrap{padding:1.05rem;display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:.85rem;overflow-y:auto}
.ac{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:.9rem;border-top:3px solid var(--red)}
.ac.full{grid-column:1/-1}
.ac h3{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.63rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.68rem;font-weight:700}
.kpi-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:.52rem}
.kpib{background:var(--pale);padding:.58rem;border-radius:2px;text-align:center;border-bottom:2px solid var(--red)}
.kpib-v{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.15rem;font-weight:700;line-height:1}
.kpib-l{font-size:.6rem;color:var(--mid);margin-top:.16rem}
/* INTELLIGENCE */
.intel-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem}
.run-check-box{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:2.2rem;border:2px dashed var(--border);border-radius:2px;color:var(--mid);cursor:pointer;transition:all .17s;text-align:center;gap:.35rem}
.run-check-box:hover{border-color:var(--red);color:var(--red)}
.alert-card{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:.82rem;border-left:4px solid var(--border);display:flex;gap:.7rem;margin-bottom:.52rem}
.alert-card.sold{border-left-color:var(--red)}.alert-card.new{border-left-color:var(--green)}
.alert-card.warn{border-left-color:var(--amber)}.alert-card.info{border-left-color:var(--blue)}
.alert-title{font-weight:600;font-size:.79rem;margin-bottom:.15rem}
.alert-meta{font-size:.69rem;color:var(--mid)}
/* MARKET PULSE */
.pulse-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem}
/* PDF QUEUE */
.queue-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.85rem}
.drop-zone{border:2px dashed var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:1.8rem;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s;background:var(--pale)}
.drop-zone:hover,.drop-zone.over{border-color:var(--red);background:var(--red-pale)}
.drop-zone input{display:none}
.queue-list{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden}
.qi{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.65rem;padding:.58rem .9rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.qi:last-child{border-bottom:none}
.qi-name{flex:1;font-size:.79rem;font-weight:500;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap}
.qi-badge{font-size:.68rem;padding:.14rem .45rem;border-radius:10px;font-weight:700}
.qi-pending{background:var(--pale);color:var(--mid)}.qi-proc{background:var(--amber-pale);color:var(--amber)}
.qi-done{background:var(--green-pale);color:var(--green)}.qi-err{background:var(--red-mid);color:var(--red)}
/* IMPORT */
.import-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.85rem}
/* DUPLICATES */
.dup-wrap{flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:1.05rem;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.7rem}
.dup-group{background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden;border-left:4px solid var(--blue)}
.dup-ghdr{padding:.58rem .9rem;background:var(--blue-pale);display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center}
.dup-ghdr h3{font-size:.78rem;font-weight:600;color:var(--blue)}
.dup-row{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.65rem;padding:.5rem .9rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.dup-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}
/* NOTES */
.notes-log{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.48rem;max-height:195px;overflow-y:auto;margin-bottom:.6rem}
.note-item{background:var(--pale);border-radius:2px;padding:.48rem .65rem;border-left:3px solid var(--red)}
.note-ts{font-size:.59rem;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.16rem}
.note-text{font-size:.78rem;line-height:1.5}
.note-row{display:flex;gap:.42rem}
.note-row textarea{flex:1;padding:.36rem .52rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.79rem;resize:none;height:48px}
.note-row textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--red)}
/* MODALS */
.overlay{display:none;position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(35,31,32,.8);z-index:500;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:1rem}
.overlay.open{display:flex}
.modal{background:var(--white);border-radius:2px;width:100%;max-width:740px;max-height:92vh;overflow-y:auto;box-shadow:0 24px 64px rgba(0,0,0,.4);border-top:3px solid var(--red)}
.modal.sm{max-width:490px}.modal.md{max-width:600px}
.mh{padding:.9rem 1.25rem .68rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);position:sticky;top:0;background:var(--white);z-index:1;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start}
.mh-title{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.1rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.03em}
.mh-sub{font-size:.68rem;color:var(--mid);margin-top:.08rem}
.mc{background:none;border:none;cursor:pointer;color:var(--mid);font-size:1rem;padding:.18rem;line-height:1}.mc:hover{color:var(--red)}
.mb{padding:.9rem 1.25rem}
.mf{padding:.68rem 1.25rem;border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;gap:.45rem;justify-content:flex-end;background:var(--pale)}
.fg{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.22rem;margin-bottom:.55rem}
.fg label{font-size:.58rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid)}
.fg input,.fg select,.fg textarea{padding:.36rem .54rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;font-size:.8rem;background:var(--white);color:var(--ink);width:100%}
.fg input:focus,.fg select:focus,.fg textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--red)}
.fg textarea{resize:vertical;min-height:54px}
.fg input[readonly]{background:var(--pale);color:var(--mid)}
.fgrid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:0 .72rem}
.fgrid3{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:0 .72rem}
.fc{grid-column:1/-1}
.fsec{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.62rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--red);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);padding-bottom:.28rem;margin:.78rem 0 .55rem}
.dg{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:.7rem;margin-bottom:.85rem}
.df{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.14rem}
.dl{font-size:.57rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid)}
.dv{font-size:.84rem;font-weight:500}
.dv.big{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.12rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--red)}
.abox{background:var(--pale);border:1px solid var(--border);border-left:3px solid var(--red);padding:.72rem;margin-top:.52rem;border-radius:2px}
.atitle{font-size:.59rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--red);margin-bottom:.34rem}
.atext{font-size:.78rem;line-height:1.65;color:var(--dark)}
.sold-hl{background:var(--green-pale);border:1px solid rgba(46,125,50,.2);border-radius:2px;padding:.72rem;margin-bottom:.82rem}
.sold-hl-t{font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.64rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--green);margin-bottom:.4rem}
/* PROPERTY IMAGE */
.prop-img-wrap{background:var(--ink);border-radius:2px;margin-bottom:.85rem;overflow:hidden;position:relative;min-height:160px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center}
.prop-img-wrap img{width:100%;height:200px;object-fit:cover;display:none}
.prop-img-links{display:flex;gap:.42rem;position:absolute;bottom:.55rem;right:.55rem}
.prop-img-link{font-size:.68rem;color:var(--white);font-weight:700;background:rgba(35,31,32,.7);padding:.22rem .55rem;border-radius:2px;text-decoration:none;backdrop-filter:blur(4px)}
.prop-img-link.red{background:var(--red)}
.prop-img-loading{font-size:.76rem;color:rgba(255,255,255,.5);display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.4rem}
/* MISC */
.spin{display:inline-block;width:11px;height:11px;border:2px solid rgba(225,37,27,.2);border-top-color:var(--red);border-radius:50%;animation:spin .6s linear infinite;vertical-align:middle;margin-right:.25rem}
@keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
.pdf-drop{border:2px dashed var(--border);border-radius:2px;padding:1.3rem;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;transition:all .15s;background:var(--pale)}
.pdf-drop:hover,.pdf-drop.over{border-color:var(--red);background:var(--red-pale)}
.pdf-drop input{display:none}
.col-map-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:.42rem;padding:.85rem;background:var(--pale);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
.cm-item{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.2rem}
.cm-item label{font-size:.57rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid)}
.cm-item select{padding:.26rem .4rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;font-size:.72rem;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif}
.import-preview-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:.73rem}
.import-preview-table th{background:var(--ink);color:var(--white);padding:.36rem .55rem;font-size:.58rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.07em;position:sticky;top:0}
.import-preview-table td{padding:.38rem .55rem;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
#toast{position:fixed;bottom:1.1rem;right:1.1rem;z-index:999;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:.28rem}
.toast-item{background:var(--ink);color:var(--white);padding:.5rem .88rem;border-radius:2px;font-size:.77rem;border-left:3px solid var(--red);animation:toastin .15s ease;max-width:265px}
.toast-item.ok{border-left-color:var(--green)}.toast-item.info{border-left-color:var(--blue)}
@keyframes toastin{from{transform:translateY(8px);opacity:0}to{transform:translateY(0);opacity:1}}
.sync-banner{position:fixed;top:52px;left:0;right:0;z-index:150;color:var(--white);text-align:center;padding:.45rem;font-size:.76rem;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif;letter-spacing:.02em;transition:background .3s}
@media(max-width:820px){nav{display:none}.analytics-wrap,.bench-grid,.league-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}.fgrid,.fgrid3,.dg{grid-template-columns:1fr}}
</style>
</head>
<body>

<header>
  <div class="logo">
    <svg class="logo-icon" viewBox="0 0 28 28" fill="none">
      <rect width="28" height="28" fill="#E1251B"/>
      <path d="M14 4L20.5 14.5L14 11L7.5 14.5L14 4Z" fill="white"/>
      <path d="M7.5 14.5L14 11V24L7.5 14.5Z" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.5)"/>
      <path d="M20.5 14.5L14 11V24L20.5 14.5Z" fill="rgba(255,255,255,.88)"/>
    </svg>
    <div class="logo-text">
      <div class="main">Cushman &amp; Wakefield</div>
      <div class="sub">NSW Sales Intelligence</div>
    </div>
  </div>
  <div class="hdr-actions">
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="runWeeklyCheck()">⚡ Intel Check</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="showView('pulse',document.getElementById('nav-pulse'))">📧 Pulse</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="exportExcel()">↓ Excel</button>
    <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="openPDFModal()">📄 PDF</button>
    <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="openAddModal()">+ Add Sale</button>
  </div>
</header>

<div class="app">
<nav>
  <div class="nav-item active" onclick="showView('database',this)" id="nav-database">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="3" y="3" width="18" height="18" rx="1"/><path d="M3 9h18M9 21V9"/></svg>Sales Database
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('tracker',this)" id="nav-tracker">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"/><path d="M12 6v6l4 2"/></svg>Campaign Tracker
    <span class="nav-badge" id="nb-tracker">0</span>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('settlement',this)" id="nav-settlement">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M9 11l3 3L22 4"/><path d="M21 12v7a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h11"/></svg>Settlement Tracker
    <span class="nav-badge blue" id="nb-settle">0</span>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-group-label">Intelligence</div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('benchmark',this)" id="nav-benchmark">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 3v18h18"/><path d="M18 17V9M13 17V5M8 17v-3"/></svg>Yield &amp; Rate Benchmark
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('agents',this)" id="nav-agents">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M17 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-4-4H5a4 4 0 0 0-4 4v2"/><circle cx="9" cy="7" r="4"/><path d="M23 21v-2a4 4 0 0 0-3-3.87M16 3.13a4 4 0 0 1 0 7.75"/></svg>Agent League Table
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('map',this)" id="nav-map">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 10c0 7-9 13-9 13s-9-6-9-13a9 9 0 0 1 18 0z"/><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="3"/></svg>Map View
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('intelligence',this)" id="nav-intelligence">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 15a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7l-4 4V5a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h14a2 2 0 0 1 2 2z"/></svg>Intelligence
    <span class="nav-badge amber" id="nb-intel" style="display:none">0</span>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('pulse',this)" id="nav-pulse">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M4 4h16c1.1 0 2 .9 2 2v12c0 1.1-.9 2-2 2H4c-1.1 0-2-.9-2-2V6c0-1.1.9-2 2-2z"/><polyline points="22,6 12,13 2,6"/></svg>Market Pulse
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('analytics',this)" id="nav-analytics">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M18 20V10M12 20V4M6 20v-6"/></svg>Analytics
  </div>
  <div class="nav-group-label">Tools</div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('queue',this)" id="nav-queue">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M14 2H6a2 2 0 0 0-2 2v16a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h12a2 2 0 0 0 2-2V8z"/><polyline points="14 2 14 8 20 8"/><line x1="12" y1="11" x2="12" y2="17"/><line x1="9" y1="14" x2="15" y2="14"/></svg>PDF Batch Queue
    <span class="nav-badge green" id="nb-queue" style="display:none">0</span>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('import',this)" id="nav-import">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><polyline points="17 8 12 3 7 8"/><line x1="12" y1="3" x2="12" y2="15"/></svg>Bulk Import
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="showView('duplicates',this)" id="nav-duplicates">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><rect x="9" y="9" width="13" height="13" rx="2"/><path d="M5 15H4a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h9a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v1"/></svg>Duplicate Detector
    <span class="nav-badge blue" id="nb-dups" style="display:none">0</span>
  </div>
  <div class="nav-item" onclick="exportExcel()">
    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M21 15v4a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-4"/><polyline points="7 10 12 15 17 10"/><line x1="12" y1="15" x2="12" y2="3"/></svg>Export to Excel
  </div>
</nav>

<div class="main">
  <!-- Stats Bar -->
  <div class="stats-bar">
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Records</div><div class="stat-val" id="st-total">0</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Total Volume</div><div class="stat-val red" id="st-vol">—</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Avg Yield</div><div class="stat-val" id="st-yield">—</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Avg Land $/m²</div><div class="stat-val" id="st-rate">—</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Active Campaigns</div><div class="stat-val amber" id="st-camps">0</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Due This Week</div><div class="stat-val red" id="st-week">0</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">In Settlement</div><div class="stat-val blue" id="st-settle">0</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Stale 90d+</div><div class="stat-val amber" id="st-stale">0</div></div><div class="sdiv"></div>
    <div class="stat"><div class="stat-lbl">Last Intel Check</div><div class="stat-val" id="st-lastcheck" style="font-size:.74rem;color:var(--mid)">Never</div></div>
  </div>

  <!-- DB Selector -->
  <div class="db-selector">
    <button class="db-btn all active" id="dbsel-all" onclick="setDB('all')">All Databases</button>
    <button class="db-btn metro" id="dbsel-Metro" onclick="setDB('Metro')">Metro</button>
    <button class="db-btn land" id="dbsel-NSWLand" onclick="setDB('NSW Land')">NSW Land</button>
    <button class="db-btn prem" id="dbsel-PremiumInvestment" onclick="setDB('Premium Investment')">Premium Investment</button>
    <span class="db-rec-count" id="db-count"></span>
  </div>

  <!-- DATABASE VIEW -->
  <div class="view active" id="view-database">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <div class="search-wrap">
        <svg class="search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35"/></svg>
        <input type="text" id="srch" placeholder="Search address, suburb, agent…" oninput="renderDB()">
      </div>
      <select class="filt" id="fClass" onchange="renderDB()"><option value="">All Asset Classes</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="fStatus" onchange="renderDB()"><option value="">All Statuses</option><option>Sold</option><option>Active</option><option value="stale">Stale 90d+</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="fProcess" onchange="renderDB()"><option value="">All Processes</option><option>Auction</option><option>EOI</option><option>Off Market</option><option>Private Treaty</option><option>Tender</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="fYear" onchange="renderDB()"><option value="">All Years</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="fRegion" onchange="renderDB()"><option value="">All Regions</option></select>
      <div class="tb-r"><span class="cnt" id="db-cnt"></span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="table-wrap">
      <table><thead><tr>
        <th>Address</th><th>Source</th><th>Asset Class</th><th>Price</th>
        <th>Sale Date</th><th>Yield</th><th>Land $/m²</th>
        <th id="th-ha" style="display:none">$/Ha</th>
        <th id="th-wale" style="display:none">WALE</th>
        <th>Agent</th><th>Status</th><th>RC</th><th></th>
      </tr></thead><tbody id="dbBody"></tbody></table>
      <div class="empty-state" id="dbEmpty" style="display:none"><div class="icon">🏢</div><strong>No records found</strong><p>Try adjusting your filters</p></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- CAMPAIGN TRACKER -->
  <div class="view" id="view-tracker">
    <div class="camp-kpis" id="campKPIs"></div>
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">Campaign Tracker</span>
      <select class="filt" id="tc-proc" onchange="renderTracker()"><option value="">All Processes</option><option>Auction</option><option>EOI</option><option>Tender</option><option>Private Treaty</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="tc-stat" onchange="renderTracker()"><option value="">All Statuses</option><option value="active">Active</option><option value="week">Due This Week</option><option value="settlement">In Settlement</option><option value="past">Past</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="tc-class" onchange="renderTracker()"><option value="">All Asset Classes</option><option>Apartment Block</option><option>Commercial</option><option>Development Site</option><option>Retail</option><option>Retail / Mixed Use</option><option>Shop Top</option><option>Industrial</option><option>Greenfield</option></select>
      <div class="search-wrap" style="max-width:180px">
        <svg class="search-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35"/></svg>
        <input type="text" id="tc-srch" placeholder="Search…" oninput="renderTracker()">
      </div>
      <div class="tb-r">
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="showView('settlement',document.getElementById('nav-settlement'))">Settlements →</button>
        <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="addCampaignModal()">+ Add Campaign</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="table-wrap">
      <table><thead><tr><th>Address</th><th>Suburb</th><th>Asset Class</th><th>Process</th><th>Close Date</th><th>Days</th><th>Status</th><th>Agent</th><th>RC Status</th><th>Actions</th></tr></thead>
      <tbody id="tcBody"></tbody></table>
      <div class="empty-state" id="tcEmpty" style="display:none"><div class="icon">📅</div><strong>No campaigns match filters</strong></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- SETTLEMENT TRACKER -->
  <div class="view" id="view-settlement">
    <div class="settle-hdr">
      <h2>🏦 Settlement Tracker</h2>
      <div class="skpi-row" id="settleKPIs"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="toolbar"><span class="toolbar-title">Properties in Settlement Period</span><div class="tb-r"><span class="cnt" id="settle-cnt"></span></div></div>
    <div class="table-wrap">
      <table><thead><tr><th>Address</th><th>Suburb</th><th>Asset Class</th><th>Sale Price</th><th>Sold Date</th><th>Settlement Date</th><th>Days</th><th>Status</th><th>Purchaser</th><th>Agent</th><th></th></tr></thead>
      <tbody id="settleBody"></tbody></table>
      <div class="empty-state" id="settleEmpty" style="display:none"><div class="icon">📋</div><strong>No properties in settlement</strong><p>Mark campaigns as sold to track settlement</p></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- BENCHMARK -->
  <div class="view" id="view-benchmark">
    <div class="toolbar"><span class="toolbar-title">⚡ Yield &amp; Rate Benchmark</span><span id="bench-db-label" style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mid);margin-left:.4rem"></span></div>
    <div class="bench-wrap">
      <div class="bench-controls">
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:150px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Suburb</label><select id="bk-suburb" onchange="renderBenchmark()"><option value="">All Suburbs</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:150px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Asset Class</label><select id="bk-class" onchange="renderBenchmark()"><option value="">All Types</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:105px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Year From</label><select id="bk-yfrom" onchange="renderBenchmark()"><option value="">All</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:105px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Year To</label><select id="bk-yto" onchange="renderBenchmark()"><option value="">All</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:105px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Min Price ($M)</label><input type="number" id="bk-minp" placeholder="0" step="0.5" oninput="renderBenchmark()"></div>
        <div class="fg" style="min-width:105px;margin-bottom:0"><label>Max Price ($M)</label><input type="number" id="bk-maxp" placeholder="Any" step="0.5" oninput="renderBenchmark()"></div>
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="clearBenchmark()" style="align-self:flex-end">Clear</button>
      </div>
      <div id="benchContent"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- AGENT LEAGUE -->
  <div class="view" id="view-agents">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">🏆 Agent League Table — Who's Selling What</span>
      <select class="filt" id="ag-region" onchange="renderAgents()"><option value="">All Regions</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="ag-class" onchange="renderAgents()"><option value="">All Asset Classes</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="ag-year" onchange="renderAgents()"><option value="">All Years</option></select>
      <div class="tb-r"><span class="cnt" id="ag-cnt"></span></div>
    </div>
    <div class="league-wrap" id="leagueWrap"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- MAP -->
  <div class="view" id="view-map">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">📍 Property Map</span>
      <select class="filt" id="map-class" onchange="renderMap()"><option value="">All Asset Classes</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="map-status" onchange="renderMap()"><option value="">All Statuses</option><option>Sold</option><option>Active</option></select>
      <select class="filt" id="map-year" onchange="renderMap()"><option value="">All Years</option></select>
      <span class="cnt" id="map-cnt"></span>
    </div>
    <div class="map-wrap"><div id="mapEl"></div></div>
  </div>

  <!-- INTELLIGENCE -->
  <div class="view" id="view-intelligence">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">Weekly Intelligence — Mon–Fri</span>
      <div class="tb-r">
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="clearAlerts()">Clear All</button>
        <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="runWeeklyCheck()">⚡ Run Check Now</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="intel-wrap" id="alertWrap"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- MARKET PULSE -->
  <div class="view" id="view-pulse">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">📧 Market Pulse — Weekly Summary Email</span>
      <div class="tb-r">
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="copyPulseHTML()">Copy HTML</button>
        <button class="btn btn-blue btn-sm" onclick="openGmailPulse()">✉ Open in Gmail</button>
        <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="generatePulse()">↻ Refresh</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="pulse-wrap" id="pulseWrap"></div>
  </div>

  <!-- ANALYTICS -->
  <div class="view" id="view-analytics"><div class="analytics-wrap" id="analyticsWrap"></div></div>

  <!-- PDF BATCH QUEUE -->
  <div class="view" id="view-queue">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">📚 PDF Batch Queue — Drop Multiple IMs</span>
      <div class="tb-r">
        <button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" id="processQBtn" onclick="processQueue()" style="display:none">▶ Process All</button>
        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="clearQueue()">Clear</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="queue-wrap">
      <div class="drop-zone" id="qDrop" onclick="document.getElementById('qFiles').click()">
        <input type="file" id="qFiles" accept=".pdf" multiple onchange="handleQueueFiles(event)">
        <div style="font-size:1.8rem;margin-bottom:.35rem">📚</div>
        <p style="font-weight:600;font-size:.86rem">Drop multiple IMs here or <span style="color:var(--red)">browse</span></p>
        <p style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--mid);margin-top:.2rem">Each PDF auto-extracts to a new sale record for review</p>
      </div>
      <div class="queue-list" id="qList" style="display:none"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- BULK IMPORT -->
  <div class="view" id="view-import">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">⬆ Bulk Import — CSV or Excel</span>
      <div class="tb-r"><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="downloadTemplate()">↓ Template CSV</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="import-wrap">
      <div class="drop-zone" id="importDrop" onclick="document.getElementById('importFile').click()">
        <input type="file" id="importFile" accept=".csv,.xlsx,.xls" onchange="handleImportFile(event)">
        <div style="font-size:1.8rem;margin-bottom:.35rem">📊</div>
        <p style="font-weight:600;font-size:.86rem">Drop CSV or Excel or <span style="color:var(--red)">browse</span></p>
        <p style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--mid);margin-top:.2rem">Map columns · Preview · Import — duplicates auto-skipped</p>
      </div>
      <div id="importPreview" style="display:none"></div>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- DUPLICATES -->
  <div class="view" id="view-duplicates">
    <div class="toolbar">
      <span class="toolbar-title">🔍 Duplicate Detector</span>
      <div class="tb-r"><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="runDupCheck(false)">↻ Scan Now</button></div>
    </div>
    <div class="dup-wrap" id="dupWrap">
      <div class="run-check-box" onclick="runDupCheck(false)">
        <div style="font-size:1.8rem">🔍</div>
        <strong style="font-size:.86rem">Click to scan for duplicates</strong>
        <p style="font-size:.78rem">Matches on address similarity, suburb, price and sale date</p>
      </div>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>
</div>

<!-- MODALS -->
<!-- ADD / EDIT SALE -->
<div class="overlay" id="addModal">
  <div class="modal">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title" id="addTitle">Add New Sale</div><div class="mh-sub">NSW Commercial · Residential Investment · Land</div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('addModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb">
      <div class="fsec">Property Details</div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="fg fc"><label>Street Address *</label><input id="f_address"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Suburb *</label><input id="f_suburb"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Region</label><select id="f_region"><option value="">—</option><option>City Fringe</option><option>Eastern Suburbs</option><option>Inner West</option><option>North Sydney</option><option>Sydney CBD</option><option>West</option><option>Western Sydney</option><option>Central Coast</option><option>Hunter</option><option>Macarthur</option><option>Riverina</option><option>Southern Highlands</option><option>Southern Tablelands</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Source Database</label><select id="f_source"><option value="">—</option><option>Metro</option><option>NSW Land</option><option>Premium Investment</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Asset Class</label><select id="f_class"><option value="">—</option><option>Apartment Block</option><option>Brownfield Infill</option><option>Co-Living/Boarding House</option><option>Commercial</option><option>Development Site</option><option>Greenfield</option><option>Hotel/Pub</option><option>Industrial</option><option>Middle Markets</option><option>Retail</option><option>Retail / Mixed Use</option><option>Service Station</option><option>Shop Top</option><option>Strata Retail</option><option>Town Centre</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Land Area (m²)</label><input type="number" id="f_land" oninput="calcA()"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Floor Area (m²)</label><input type="number" id="f_floor"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Primary Zoning</label><input id="f_zoning"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>FSR</label><input type="number" id="f_fsr" step="0.1"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Height (m)</label><input type="number" id="f_height" step="0.5"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Configuration</label><input id="f_config"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Constraints</label><input id="f_constraints"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Dev Stage</label><select id="f_devstage"><option value="">—</option><option>Raw</option><option>DA Lodged</option><option>DA Approved</option><option>HDA Lodged</option><option>HDA Approved</option><option>SSD Lodged</option><option>SSD Approved</option><option>Civils Completed</option></select></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fsec">Transaction Details</div>
      <div class="fgrid3">
        <div class="fg"><label>Sale Price ($)</label><input type="number" id="f_price" oninput="calcA()"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Price Guidance ($) <span style="font-size:.55rem;color:var(--amber);font-weight:700">ACTIVE</span></label><input type="number" id="f_priceguide" placeholder="e.g. 8500000"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Status</label><select id="f_status"><option>Active</option><option>Sold</option><option>Withdrawn</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Sale Date</label><input type="date" id="f_date"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Settlement Date</label><input type="date" id="f_settlementdate"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Process</label><select id="f_process"><option value="">—</option><option>Auction</option><option>EOI</option><option>Off Market</option><option>Private Treaty</option><option>Tender</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Campaign Close Date</label><input type="date" id="f_closedate"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Net Income ($/yr)</label><input type="number" id="f_netrent" oninput="calcA()"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Outgoings ($/yr)</label><input type="number" id="f_outgoings"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Yield (%) — auto</label><input type="number" id="f_yield" step="0.01" readonly></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Land $/m² — auto</label><input type="number" id="f_landrate" readonly></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>WALE (yrs)</label><input type="number" id="f_wale" step="0.1"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Purchaser</label><input id="f_purchaser"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Vendor</label><input id="f_vendor"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fsec">Agency</div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="fg"><label>Agent</label><input id="f_agent"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Co-Agent</label><input id="f_coagent"></div>
        <div class="fg fc"><label>Notes / Comments</label><textarea id="f_comments"></textarea></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('addModal')">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-red" id="saveBtn" onclick="saveSale()">Save Sale</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- DETAIL MODAL -->
<div class="overlay" id="detailModal">
  <div class="modal">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title" id="detTitle">Sale Detail</div><div class="mh-sub" id="detSub"></div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('detailModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb" id="detBody"></div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" id="detDel" style="color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)">Delete</button><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('detailModal')">Close</button><button class="btn btn-red" id="detEdit">Edit Sale</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- MARK SOLD -->
<div class="overlay" id="soldModal">
  <div class="modal md">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title">Mark as Sold</div><div class="mh-sub" id="soldSub"></div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('soldModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb">
      <div class="sold-hl"><div class="sold-hl-t">🎉 Campaign Result</div><div id="soldInfo" style="font-size:.8rem"></div></div>
      <div class="fsec">Sale Details</div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="fg"><label>Sale Price ($) *</label><input type="number" id="s_price" oninput="calcSY()"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Sold Date *</label><input type="date" id="s_solddate"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Purchaser</label><input id="s_purchaser"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Vendor</label><input id="s_vendor"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Net Income ($/yr)</label><input type="number" id="s_netrent" oninput="calcSY()"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Yield (%) — auto</label><input type="number" id="s_yield" readonly step="0.01"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fsec">Settlement</div>
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="fg"><label>Settlement Date *</label><input type="date" id="s_settle"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Days</label><select id="s_days" onchange="autoSettle()"><option value="42">42 days (Standard)</option><option value="30">30 days</option><option value="60">60 days</option><option value="90">90 days</option><option value="custom">Custom</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg fc"><label>Result Notes</label><textarea id="s_notes" placeholder="e.g. Sold under hammer, 8 bidders…"></textarea></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('soldModal')">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-green" onclick="confirmSold()">✓ Confirm Sale &amp; Track Settlement</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ADD CAMPAIGN -->
<div class="overlay" id="campModal">
  <div class="modal sm">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title">Add Campaign</div><div class="mh-sub">Track upcoming auction, EOI or tender</div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('campModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb">
      <div class="fgrid">
        <div class="fg fc"><label>Address *</label><input id="ca_addr"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Suburb</label><input id="ca_suburb"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Asset Class</label><select id="ca_class"><option value="">—</option><option>Apartment Block</option><option>Commercial</option><option>Development Site</option><option>Retail</option><option>Retail / Mixed Use</option><option>Shop Top</option><option>Industrial</option><option>Greenfield</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Process *</label><select id="ca_process"><option>Auction</option><option>EOI</option><option>Tender</option><option>Private Treaty</option></select></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Campaign Close Date *</label><input type="date" id="ca_date"></div>
        <div class="fg"><label>Agent</label><input id="ca_agent"></div>
        <div class="fg fc"><label>Notes / Guide Price / Income</label><textarea id="ca_notes" rows="3"></textarea></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('campModal')">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-red" onclick="saveCampaign()">Save Campaign</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- PDF SINGLE EXTRACT -->
<div class="overlay" id="pdfModal">
  <div class="modal sm">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title">Extract from PDF</div><div class="mh-sub">Upload an IM — AI extracts all key details</div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('pdfModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb">
      <div class="pdf-drop" id="pdfDrop" onclick="document.getElementById('pdfFile').click()">
        <input type="file" id="pdfFile" accept=".pdf" onchange="handlePDF(event)">
        <div style="font-size:1.8rem;margin-bottom:.35rem">📄</div>
        <p style="font-weight:600">Drop PDF or <span style="color:var(--red)">browse</span></p>
        <p style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mid);margin-top:.18rem">For multiple PDFs use the Batch Queue</p>
      </div>
      <div id="pdfStatus"></div><div id="pdfPreview"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('pdfModal')">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-red" id="pdfUseBtn" style="display:none" onclick="usePDF()">Use Extracted Data →</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- COLUMN MAP -->
<div class="overlay" id="colMapModal">
  <div class="modal">
    <div class="mh"><div><div class="mh-title">Map Columns</div><div class="mh-sub" id="colMapSub">Match your file columns to database fields</div></div><button class="mc" onclick="closeM('colMapModal')">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mb" id="colMapBody"></div>
    <div class="mf"><button class="btn btn-outline" onclick="closeM('colMapModal')">Cancel</button><button class="btn btn-red" onclick="commitImport()">Import Records</button></div>
  </div>
</div>

<div id="toast"></div>

<script>
// Global error handler — prevent any error from killing the whole app
window.onerror = function(msg, src, line, col, err) {
  console.warn('Caught error:', msg, 'at', src, line);
  return true; // Prevent default error handling
};
window.onunhandledrejection = function(e) {
  console.warn('Unhandled rejection:', e.reason);
  e.preventDefault();
};
</script>
