// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// GOOGLE SHEETS SYNC — via Apps Script (JSONP, no CORS issues)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const GS_URL = 'https://script.google.com/macros/s/AKfycbwG1iwVmj25KuTyH0nNCIboHhXRcLojCrBkDINza9CbwsoZxniq4o_bRSldjIJyWti6gw/exec';
const GS_NEW_SHEET_ID = '1j6ocLOMoNKuaFGClWaNPSa7NxcFYeANUszwJWldNipk';
let gsReady = false;

// JSONP fetch — bypasses CORS completely, handles GAS redirects
function jsonpFetch(url, timeoutMs=15000) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const cbName = 'cb_' + Math.random().toString(36).substr(2,9);
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    let settled = false;
    const timer = setTimeout(() => {
      if(!settled) { settled=true; cleanup(); reject(new Error('Timeout')); }
    }, timeoutMs);
    window[cbName] = (data) => {
      if(!settled) { settled=true; clearTimeout(timer); cleanup(); resolve(data); }
    };
    function cleanup() { delete window[cbName]; if(script.parentNode) script.remove(); }
    script.onerror = () => {
      // Suppress cross-origin script errors — GAS redirect causes these
      // Wait briefly to see if callback fires anyway
      setTimeout(() => { 
        if(!settled) { settled=true; cleanup(); reject(new Error('GS unreachable')); } 
      }, 2000);
    };
    // Prevent cross-origin script errors from propagating to window
    script.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
    script.src = url + (url.includes('?')?'&':'?') + 'callback=' + cbName;
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });
}

// POST via no-cors (fire and forget — write still happens server-side)
async function gsPost(action, rows) {
  try {
    fetch(GS_URL, {
      method:'POST', mode:'no-cors',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({action, rows})
    });
    await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,800));
    return {ok:true};
  } catch(e) { return {ok:false, error:e.message}; }
}

// Column definitions
const GS_SALES_COLS = [
  'ID','Source Database','Address','Suburb','Region','Classification','Status',
  'Sale Price','Price Guidance','Net Rent','Outgoings','Yield','Date Listed',
  'Sale Date','Settlement Date','Process','Campaign Close Date','RC Status',
  'Land Area (m2)','Land $ / m2','Floor Area (m2)','Capital Value (m2)',
  'Units / Rooms','$ / Unit','WALE','Parking','Configuration',
  'Land Area (Ha)','Land $ / Ha','Land Area (Acre)','Land $ / Acre',
  'No Lots or Units','Lot or Unit Rate ($)','Permissible GFA','GFA $/SQM',
  'Primary Zoning','Secondary Zoning','FSR','Height (m)','Approval (m2)',
  'Development Stage','Constraints','Agent','Co Agent','Vendor','Purchaser','Comments'
];
const GS_CAMP_COLS = ['id','address','suburb','classification','process','closeDate',
  'agent','notes','status','salePrice','purchaser','soldDate','settlementDate','rcStatus'];

function gsRecToRow(rec, cols) {
  return cols.map(c => {
    const aliases = {
      'ID':['id','ID'],'Source Database':['Source Database','source'],
      'Address':['address','Address'],'Suburb':['suburb','Suburb'],
      'Region':['region','Region'],'Classification':['classification','Classification'],
      'Status':['status','Status'],'Sale Price':['price','Sale Price'],
      'Price Guidance':['priceGuide','Price Guidance'],'Net Rent':['netRent','Net Rent'],
      'Outgoings':['outgoings'],'Yield':['yld','Yield'],
      'Sale Date':['saleDate','Sale Date'],'Settlement Date':['settlementDate','Settlement Date'],
      'Process':['process','Process'],'Campaign Close Date':['campaignClose','Campaign Close Date'],
      'RC Status':['rcStatus','RC Status'],'Land Area (m2)':['landArea','Land Area (m2)'],
      'Land $ / m2':['landRate','Land $ / m2'],'Floor Area (m2)':['floorArea','Floor Area (m2)'],
      'WALE':['wale','WALE'],'Agent':['agent','Agent'],'Co Agent':['coAgent','Co Agent'],
      'Vendor':['vendor','Vendor'],'Purchaser':['purchaser','Purchaser'],
      'Primary Zoning':['zoning','Primary Zoning'],'FSR':['fsr','FSR'],
      'Height (m)':['height','Height (m)'],'Development Stage':['devStage','Development Stage'],
      'Constraints':['constraints','Constraints'],'Comments':['comments','Comments'],
      'Configuration':['configuration','Configuration'],
      'Land Area (Ha)':['Land Area (Ha)'],'Land $ / Ha':['Land $ / Ha'],
    };
    const keys = aliases[c] || [c];
    for(const k of keys) { const v=rec[k]; if(v!==null&&v!==undefined&&v!=='') return String(v); }
    return '';
  });
}

async function gsPushAllSales() {
  const rows = [GS_SALES_COLS, ...sales.map(s=>gsRecToRow(s,GS_SALES_COLS))];
  return gsPost('writeSales', rows);
}
async function gsPushAllCamps() {
  const rows = [GS_CAMP_COLS, ...campaigns.map(c=>gsRecToRow(c,GS_CAMP_COLS))];
  return gsPost('writeCamps', rows);
}

const SALES_NUM = ['Sale Price','Price Guidance','Net Rent','Outgoings','Yield',
  'Land Area (m2)','Land $ / m2','Floor Area (m2)','WALE','Land Area (Ha)','Land $ / Ha','FSR'];

async function gsReadSales() {
  const json = await jsonpFetch(GS_URL + '?action=readSales');
  if(!json.ok) throw new Error(json.error);
  const rows = json.data;
  if(!rows || rows.length < 2) return null;
  const headers = rows[0].map(h=>String(h));
  return rows.slice(1).map(row => {
    const rec = {};
    headers.forEach((h,i) => {
      let v = (row[i]===undefined||row[i]===null)?'':String(row[i]);
      if(SALES_NUM.includes(h)) rec[h]=v===''?null:(parseFloat(v)||null);
      else rec[h]=v;
    });
    // camelCase aliases for compatibility
    rec.id=rec['ID']||gId(); rec.source=rec['Source Database']||'Metro';
    rec.address=rec['Address']||''; rec.suburb=rec['Suburb']||'';
    rec.region=rec['Region']||''; rec.classification=rec['Classification']||'';
    rec.status=rec['Status']||'Active'; rec.price=rec['Sale Price']||null;
    rec.priceGuide=rec['Price Guidance']||null; rec.netRent=rec['Net Rent']||null;
    rec.yld=rec['Yield']||null; rec.saleDate=rec['Sale Date']||'';
    rec.settlementDate=rec['Settlement Date']||''; rec.process=rec['Process']||'';
    rec.campaignClose=rec['Campaign Close Date']||''; rec.rcStatus=rec['RC Status']||'';
    rec.landArea=rec['Land Area (m2)']||null; rec.landRate=rec['Land $ / m2']||null;
    rec.floorArea=rec['Floor Area (m2)']||null; rec.wale=rec['WALE']||null;
    rec.agent=rec['Agent']||''; rec.coAgent=rec['Co Agent']||'';
    rec.vendor=rec['Vendor']||''; rec.purchaser=rec['Purchaser']||'';
    rec.zoning=rec['Primary Zoning']||''; rec.fsr=rec['FSR']||null;
    rec.height=rec['Height (m)']||null; rec.devStage=rec['Development Stage']||'';
    rec.constraints=rec['Constraints']||''; rec.comments=rec['Comments']||'';
    rec.configuration=rec['Configuration']||'';
    return rec;
  }).filter(r=>r.address||r['Address']);
}

async function gsReadCamps() {
  const json = await jsonpFetch(GS_URL + '?action=readCamps');
  if(!json.ok) throw new Error(json.error);
  const rows = json.data;
  if(!rows||rows.length<2) return null;
  const headers=rows[0].map(h=>String(h));
  return rows.slice(1).map(row=>{
    const rec={};
    headers.forEach((h,i)=>{rec[h]=row[i]??'';});
    return rec;
  }).filter(r=>r.address||r.id);
}

async function gsSyncIfReady() {
  if(!gsReady) return;
  try { await gsPushAllSales(); await gsPushAllCamps(); } catch(e) { console.warn('GS sync:',e.message); }
}

async function gsManualSync() {
  const btn=event?.target;
  if(btn){btn.textContent='⟳';btn.disabled=true;}
  try {
    await gsPushAllSales(); await gsPushAllCamps();
    toast('✓ Synced — '+sales.length+' records','ok');
  } catch(e) { toast('Sync failed: '+e.message); }
  if(btn){btn.textContent='🔄 Sync';btn.disabled=false;}
}

function showBanner(msg, color='#1565C0', duration=5000) {
  let b=document.getElementById('sync-banner');
  if(!b){b=document.createElement('div');b.id='sync-banner';b.className='sync-banner';document.body.appendChild(b);}
  b.style.background=color; b.textContent=msg;
  if(duration) setTimeout(()=>b.remove(),duration);
}

async function gsLoad() {
  // Always show seed data first — instant, never fails
  try { render(); populateFilters(); populateBenchmarkSuburbs(); runDupCheck(true); } catch(e) { console.warn('Render error:', e); }

  showBanner('⟳ Syncing database…','#1565C0',0);
  try {
    const sheetSales = await gsReadSales();
    if(sheetSales && sheetSales.length > 0) {
      sales = sheetSales;
      const sheetCamps = await gsReadCamps();
      if(sheetCamps && sheetCamps.length > 0) campaigns = sheetCamps;
      render(); populateFilters(); populateBenchmarkSuburbs();
      showBanner('✓ '+sales.length+' records — database live','#2E7D32',5000);
    } else {
      showBanner('⟳ First run — uploading '+sales.length+' records…','#B8860B',0);
      await gsPushAllSales(); await gsPushAllCamps();
      showBanner('✓ '+sales.length+' records uploaded — database live','#2E7D32',5000);
    }
    gsReady = true;
  } catch(e) {
    showBanner('✓ '+sales.length+' records loaded','#2E7D32',4000);
    gsReady = true; // allow writes even if read fails
    console.warn('GS load:',e.message);
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// STATE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
let sales=SEED_RECORDS.map(x=>({...x}));
let campaigns=SEED_CAMPAIGNS.map(x=>({...x}));
let alerts=[], notes={}, meta={lastCheck:null};
let editId=null, soldCampId=null, pdfData=null;
let importRows=[], importHeaders=[];
let queueFiles=[];
let mapInst=null, mapMarkers=[];
let activeDB='all';

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// UTILITIES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const f$=n=>n?'$'+Number(n).toLocaleString('en-AU',{maximumFractionDigits:0}):'—';
const fM=n=>n?'$'+Math.round(n).toLocaleString()+'/m²':'—';
const fD=s=>{if(!s||s===''||s==='SOLD')return'—';try{return new Date(s).toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'});}catch{return s;}};
const gId=()=>Math.random().toString(36).substr(2,9);
const today=()=>new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
const daysTo=d=>{if(!d)return null;return Math.round((new Date(d)-new Date())/86400000);};
const addDays=(d,n)=>{const dt=new Date(d);dt.setDate(dt.getDate()+n);return dt.toISOString().split('T')[0];};
const yldD=y=>!y?'—':(y<1?(y*100).toFixed(2):Number(y).toFixed(2))+'%';
const avg=arr=>arr.length?arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+b)/arr.length:null;
const med=arr=>{if(!arr.length)return null;const s=[...arr].sort((a,b)=>a-b);return s[Math.floor(s.length/2)];};
const getF=(s,k,...alts)=>{for(const a of[k,...alts]){const v=s[a];if(v!==null&&v!==undefined&&v!=='')return v;}return null;};
const getSrc=s=>s['Source Database']||s.source||'Metro';
const getAddr=s=>s.address||s['Address']||'';
const getSuburb=s=>s.suburb||s['Suburb']||'';
const getRegion=s=>s.region||s['Region']||'';
const getCls=s=>s.classification||s['Classification']||'';
const getStatus=s=>s.status||s['Status']||'Active';
const getPrice=s=>s.price||s['Sale Price']||null;
const getYld=s=>s.yld||s['Yield']||null;
const getLR=s=>s.landRate||s['Land $ / m2']||null;
const getAgent=s=>s.agent||s['Agent']||'';
const getSd=s=>s.saleDate||s['Sale Date']||'';
const getCC=s=>s.campaignClose||s['Campaign Close Date']||'';
const getRC=s=>s.rcStatus||s['RC Status']||'';
const getPG=s=>s.priceGuide||s['Price Guidance']||null;
const isStale=s=>{const st=getStatus(s);const cc=getCC(s);return st!=='Sold'&&cc&&daysTo(cc)<-90;};
const srcCls=src=>src==='NSW Land'?'land':src==='Premium Investment'?'prem':'metro';

function calcA(){
  const p=parseFloat(document.getElementById('f_price').value)||0;
  const r=parseFloat(document.getElementById('f_netrent').value)||0;
  const l=parseFloat(document.getElementById('f_land').value)||0;
  document.getElementById('f_yield').value=(p&&r)?((r/p)*100).toFixed(2):'';
  document.getElementById('f_landrate').value=(p&&l)?Math.round(p/l):'';
}
function calcSY(){
  const p=parseFloat(document.getElementById('s_price').value)||0;
  const r=parseFloat(document.getElementById('s_netrent').value)||0;
  document.getElementById('s_yield').value=(p&&r)?((r/p)*100).toFixed(2):'';
}
function autoSettle(){
  const sel=document.getElementById('s_days').value;
  const sd=document.getElementById('s_solddate').value;
  if(sel==='custom'||!sd)return;
  document.getElementById('s_settle').value=addDays(sd,parseInt(sel));
}

function toast(msg,type=''){
  const t=document.getElementById('toast');
  const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='toast-item'+(type?' '+type:'');el.textContent=msg;
  t.appendChild(el);setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),3500);
}
function closeM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.remove('open');}
function openM(id){document.getElementById(id).classList.add('open');}

function showView(name,el){
  document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v=>v.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-item').forEach(n=>n.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('view-'+name).classList.add('active');
  if(el)el.classList.add('active');
  const fns={analytics:renderAnalytics,tracker:renderTracker,settlement:renderSettlement,
    benchmark:renderBenchmark,agents:renderAgents,map:initMap,
    intelligence:renderIntelligence,pulse:generatePulse,duplicates:()=>runDupCheck(false)};
  if(fns[name])fns[name]();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DATABASE SELECTOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function setDB(db) {
  activeDB=db;
  ['all','Metro','NSWLand','PremiumInvestment'].forEach(k=>{
    const b=document.getElementById('dbsel-'+k);if(b)b.classList.remove('active');
  });
  const keyMap={'all':'all','Metro':'Metro','NSW Land':'NSWLand','Premium Investment':'PremiumInvestment'};
  const el=document.getElementById('dbsel-'+(keyMap[db]||db));
  if(el)el.classList.add('active');
  const isL=db==='NSW Land',isP=db==='Premium Investment';
  const ha=document.getElementById('th-ha'),wa=document.getElementById('th-wale');
  if(ha)ha.style.display=isL?'':'none';
  if(wa)wa.style.display=isP?'':'none';
  populateFilters();
  renderDB();updateStats();renderTracker();
  populateBenchmarkSuburbs();
}

function getFilteredSales(){
  if(activeDB==='all')return sales;
  return sales.filter(s=>getSrc(s)===activeDB);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// POPULATE FILTERS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const CLASS_SETS={
  'Metro':['Apartment Block','Car Park','Co-Living/Boarding House','Commercial','Development Site','Hotel/Pub','Industrial','Middle Markets','Retail','Shop Top','Strata Retail'],
  'NSW Land':['Brownfield Infill','Development Site','Greenfield','Masterplanned Community','Town Centre'],
  'Premium Investment':['Service Station','Childcare','Medical','Fast Food','Retail Net Lease'],
  'all':['Apartment Block','Brownfield Infill','Car Park','Co-Living/Boarding House','Commercial','Development Site','Greenfield','Hotel/Pub','Industrial','Masterplanned Community','Middle Markets','Retail','Service Station','Shop Top','Strata Retail','Town Centre'],
};
const REGION_SETS={
  'Metro':['City Fringe','Eastern Suburbs','Inner West','North Sydney','Sydney CBD','West'],
  'NSW Land':['Central Coast','Hunter','Inner West','Macarthur','Riverina','Southern Highlands','Southern Tablelands','Western Sydney'],
  'Premium Investment':['Eastern Suburbs','Inner West','North Sydney','Western Sydney','West'],
  'all':['Central Coast','City Fringe','Eastern Suburbs','Hunter','Inner West','Macarthur','North Sydney','Riverina','Southern Highlands','Southern Tablelands','Sydney CBD','West','Western Sydney'],
};

function populateFilters(){
  const classes=CLASS_SETS[activeDB]||CLASS_SETS.all;
  const regions=REGION_SETS[activeDB]||REGION_SETS.all;
  const yrs=[...new Set(getFilteredSales().map(s=>getSd(s)?.substring(0,4)).filter(Boolean))].sort().reverse();

  ['fClass','bk-class','ag-class','map-class'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;
    const cur=el.value;
    el.innerHTML='<option value="">All Asset Classes</option>'+classes.map(c=>`<option${c===cur?' selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('');
  });
  ['fRegion','ag-region'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;
    const cur=el.value;
    el.innerHTML='<option value="">All Regions</option>'+regions.map(r=>`<option${r===cur?' selected':''}>${r}</option>`).join('');
  });
  ['fYear','bk-yfrom','bk-yto','ag-year','map-year'].forEach(id=>{
    const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;
    const cur=el.value;
    const lbl=id.includes('yfrom')||id.includes('yto')?'All':'All Years';
    el.innerHTML=`<option value="">${lbl}</option>`+yrs.map(y=>`<option${y===cur?' selected':''}>${y}</option>`).join('');
  });
  // Update db count
  const dc=document.getElementById('db-count');
  if(dc)dc.textContent=getFilteredSales().length+' records'+(activeDB==='all'?' across all databases':' — '+activeDB);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MAIN RENDER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function render(){renderDB();updateStats();renderTracker();renderSettlement();updateBadges();}

function updateBadges(){
  const ac=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0);
  const is=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement');
  const ua=alerts.filter(a=>!a.read);
  const stale=sales.filter(isStale).length;
  document.getElementById('nb-tracker').textContent=ac.length;
  document.getElementById('nb-settle').textContent=is.length;
  document.getElementById('st-stale').textContent=stale;
  const ni=document.getElementById('nb-intel');ni.textContent=ua.length;ni.style.display=ua.length?'':'none';
  const nq=document.getElementById('nb-queue');nq.textContent=queueFiles.length;nq.style.display=queueFiles.length?'':'none';
}

function updateStats(){
  const fs=getFilteredSales();
  const wp=fs.filter(s=>getPrice(s)>0);const vol=wp.reduce((a,s)=>a+getPrice(s),0);
  const yields=fs.filter(s=>getYld(s)).map(s=>{const y=getYld(s);return y<1?y*100:parseFloat(y);});
  const rates=fs.filter(s=>getLR(s)>500).map(s=>getLR(s));
  const ac=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0);
  const dw=ac.filter(c=>daysTo(c.closeDate)<=7);
  document.getElementById('st-total').textContent=fs.length;
  document.getElementById('st-vol').textContent=vol>0?f$(vol):'—';
  document.getElementById('st-yield').textContent=avg(yields)?avg(yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—';
  document.getElementById('st-rate').textContent=avg(rates)?'$'+Math.round(avg(rates)).toLocaleString()+'/m²':'—';
  document.getElementById('st-camps').textContent=ac.length;
  document.getElementById('st-week').textContent=dw.length;
  document.getElementById('st-settle').textContent=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement').length;
  document.getElementById('st-lastcheck').textContent=meta.lastCheck?new Date(meta.lastCheck).toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short'}):'Never';
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DATABASE VIEW
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function renderDB(){
  const srch=(document.getElementById('srch')||{value:''}).value.toLowerCase();
  const fC=(document.getElementById('fClass')||{value:''}).value;
  const fS=(document.getElementById('fStatus')||{value:''}).value;
  const fP=(document.getElementById('fProcess')||{value:''}).value;
  const fY=(document.getElementById('fYear')||{value:''}).value;
  const fR=(document.getElementById('fRegion')||{value:''}).value;
  const isL=activeDB==='NSW Land',isP=activeDB==='Premium Investment';

  const fil=getFilteredSales().filter(s=>{
    const txt=[getAddr(s),getSuburb(s),getAgent(s),getCls(s),getRegion(s),s.purchaser||s['Purchaser']||''].join(' ').toLowerCase();
    if(srch&&!txt.includes(srch))return false;
    if(fC&&getCls(s)!==fC)return false;
    if(fS==='stale')return isStale(s);
    if(fS&&getStatus(s)!==fS)return false;
    if(fP&&(s.process||s['Process'])!==fP)return false;
    if(fY&&(!getSd(s)||!getSd(s).startsWith(fY)))return false;
    if(fR&&getRegion(s)!==fR)return false;
    return true;
  });

  document.getElementById('db-cnt').textContent=fil.length+' of '+getFilteredSales().length+' records';
  const empty=document.getElementById('dbEmpty');
  empty.style.display=fil.length===0?'flex':'none';

  const src=getSrc;
  document.getElementById('dbBody').innerHTML=fil.map(s=>{
    const nc=(notes[s.id]||[]).length;
    const nBadge=nc?`<span style="background:var(--blue-pale);color:var(--blue);border-radius:10px;padding:.06rem .32rem;font-size:.56rem;font-weight:700;margin-left:.28rem">${nc}</span>`:'';
    const stale=isStale(s);
    const status=getStatus(s);
    const rc=getRC(s);
    const rcBadge=rc?`<span class="badge" style="font-size:.55rem;background:${rc==='Sold'?'var(--green-pale)':rc==='Active'?'var(--red-mid)':'var(--pale)'};color:${rc==='Sold'?'var(--green)':rc==='Active'?'var(--red)':'var(--mid)'}">${rc}</span>`:'<span style="color:var(--light);font-size:.68rem">—</span>';
    const pg=getPG(s);
    const haVal=s['Land $ / Ha'];
    const waleVal=s.wale||s['WALE'];
    const dbSrc=getSrc(s);
    return `<tr onclick="openDetail('${s.id||s['ID']}')">
      <td class="t-addr"><div>${getAddr(s)}${nBadge}</div><div class="sub">${getSuburb(s)}${getRegion(s)?' · '+getRegion(s):''}</div></td>
      <td><span class="db-tag ${srcCls(dbSrc)}">${dbSrc==='Premium Investment'?'Premium':dbSrc==='NSW Land'?'Land':dbSrc}</span></td>
      <td style="font-size:.75rem">${getCls(s)||'—'}</td>
      <td class="mono">${f$(getPrice(s))}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.75rem">${fD(getSd(s))}</td>
      <td class="yv">${yldD(getYld(s))}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.74rem">${fM(getLR(s))}</td>
      ${isL?`<td style="font-size:.73rem;color:var(--land);font-weight:600">${haVal?'$'+Math.round(haVal).toLocaleString()+'/Ha':'—'}</td>`:''}
      ${isP?`<td style="font-size:.73rem;font-weight:600">${waleVal?waleVal+' yrs':'—'}</td>`:''}
      <td style="font-size:.71rem;color:var(--mid)">${getAgent(s)||'—'}</td>
      <td>${stale?'<span class="badge b-stale">⚠ Stale</span> ':''}<span class="badge b-${status.toLowerCase()}">${status}</span>${status!=='Sold'&&pg?` <span class="guide-tag">💰 ${f$(pg)}</span>`:''}</td>
      <td>${rcBadge}</td>
      <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="editSale('${s.id||s['ID']}')">Edit</button></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CAMPAIGN TRACKER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function renderTracker(){
  const tcP=(document.getElementById('tc-proc')||{value:''}).value;
  const tcS=(document.getElementById('tc-stat')||{value:''}).value;
  const tcC=(document.getElementById('tc-class')||{value:''}).value;
  const tcQ=((document.getElementById('tc-srch')||{value:''}).value||'').toLowerCase();

  const filtered=campaigns.filter(c=>{
    if(c.status==='sold-complete')return false;
    if(tcP&&c.process!==tcP)return false;
    if(tcC&&c.classification!==tcC)return false;
    if(tcQ&&!(c.address+' '+(c.suburb||'')).toLowerCase().includes(tcQ))return false;
    if(tcS){
      const d=daysTo(c.closeDate);
      if(tcS==='active'&&!(c.status==='active'&&d!==null&&d>7))return false;
      if(tcS==='week'&&!(c.status==='active'&&d!==null&&d>=0&&d<=7))return false;
      if(tcS==='settlement'&&c.status!=='settlement')return false;
      if(tcS==='past'&&!(c.status==='active'&&d!==null&&d<0))return false;
    }
    return true;
  });

  const sorted=[...filtered].sort((a,b)=>{
    if(a.status==='settlement'&&b.status!=='settlement')return 1;
    if(b.status==='settlement'&&a.status!=='settlement')return -1;
    const da=daysTo(a.closeDate),db=daysTo(b.closeDate);
    if(da===null&&db===null)return 0;if(da===null)return 1;if(db===null)return -1;
    if(da>=0&&db>=0)return da-db;if(da>=0)return -1;if(db>=0)return 1;return db-da;
  });

  const allAc=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0);
  const allIs=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement');
  const allPast=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)<0);
  const allDW=allAc.filter(c=>daysTo(c.closeDate)<=7);

  document.getElementById('campKPIs').innerHTML=[
    {l:'Active',v:allAc.length,c:''},{l:'Due This Week',v:allDW.length,c:'amber'},
    {l:'In Settlement',v:allIs.length,c:'green'},{l:'Past / Awaiting',v:allPast.length,c:''},
    {l:'Total Tracked',v:campaigns.length,c:'red'},
  ].map(k=>`<div class="ckpi"><div class="ckpi-l">${k.l}</div><div class="ckpi-v ${k.c}">${k.v}</div></div>`).join('');

  document.getElementById('tcEmpty').style.display=sorted.length===0?'flex':'none';
  document.getElementById('tcBody').innerHTML=sorted.map(c=>{
    const days=c.status==='settlement'?daysTo(c.settlementDate):daysTo(c.closeDate);
    let dc='dp-past',dt='—',sc='sp-past',st='Past';
    if(c.status==='settlement'){dc='dp-settle';dt=days!==null?(days>=0?days+'d':'Settled'):'—';sc='sp-settle';st='Settlement';}
    else if(days!==null){
      if(days===0){dc='dp-today';dt='TODAY';sc='sp-today';st='TODAY!';}
      else if(days>0&&days<=7){dc='dp-soon';dt=days+'d';sc='sp-week';st='This Week';}
      else if(days>7){dc='dp-active';dt=days+'d';sc='sp-active';st='Active';}
      else{dt=Math.abs(days)+'d ago';}
    }
    const rc=c.rcStatus?`<span class="badge" style="font-size:.55rem">${c.rcStatus}</span>`:'<span style="color:var(--light);font-size:.68rem">—</span>';
    const soldBtn=c.status==='active'?`<button class="msb" onclick="event.stopPropagation();openSoldModal('${c.id}')">Mark Sold</button>`:'';
    return `<tr>
      <td style="font-weight:600;font-size:.79rem">${c.address}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--mid)">${c.suburb||'—'}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.72rem">${c.classification||'—'}</td>
      <td><span class="badge" style="background:rgba(35,31,32,.07);color:var(--dark)">${c.process||'—'}</span></td>
      <td style="font-size:.74rem">${c.status==='settlement'?fD(c.settlementDate):fD(c.closeDate)}</td>
      <td><span class="dpill ${dc}">${dt}</span></td>
      <td><span class="spill ${sc}">${st}</span></td>
      <td style="font-size:.71rem;color:var(--mid)">${c.agent||'—'}</td>
      <td>${rc}</td>
      <td onclick="event.stopPropagation()"><div style="display:flex;gap:.24rem">${soldBtn}<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color:var(--mid)" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteCamp('${c.id}')">×</button></div></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SETTLEMENT TRACKER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function renderSettlement(){
  const inS=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement'||c.status==='settled');
  const dw=inS.filter(c=>{const d=daysTo(c.settlementDate);return d!==null&&d>=0&&d<=7;});
  const setd=inS.filter(c=>{const d=daysTo(c.settlementDate);return d!==null&&d<0;});
  document.getElementById('settleKPIs').innerHTML=[
    {l:'In Settlement',v:inS.length},{l:'Settling This Week',v:dw.length},{l:'Settled',v:setd.length},
  ].map(k=>`<div class="skpi"><div class="skpi-v">${k.v}</div><div class="skpi-l">${k.l}</div></div>`).join('');
  document.getElementById('settle-cnt').textContent=inS.length+' properties';
  document.getElementById('settleEmpty').style.display=inS.length===0?'flex':'none';
  const sorted=[...inS].sort((a,b)=>(daysTo(a.settlementDate)||999)-(daysTo(b.settlementDate)||999));
  document.getElementById('settleBody').innerHTML=sorted.map(c=>{
    const days=daysTo(c.settlementDate);
    let dc='dp-active',dt=days!==null?days+'d':'—',sc='sp-active',st='In Settlement';
    if(days!==null){if(days<0){dc='dp-past';dt='Settled';sc='sp-past';st='Settled';}else if(days===0){dc='dp-today';dt='TODAY';sc='sp-today';st='TODAY!';}else if(days<=7){dc='dp-soon';sc='sp-week';st='Due This Week';}}
    return `<tr>
      <td style="font-weight:600">${c.address}</td>
      <td style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.74rem">${c.suburb||'—'}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.74rem">${c.classification||'—'}</td>
      <td class="mono">${f$(c.salePrice)}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.74rem">${fD(c.soldDate)}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.74rem">${fD(c.settlementDate)}</td>
      <td><span class="dpill ${dc} dp-settle">${dt}</span></td>
      <td><span class="spill ${sc}">${st}</span></td>
      <td style="font-size:.71rem;color:var(--mid)">${c.purchaser||'—'}</td>
      <td style="font-size:.71rem;color:var(--mid)">${c.agent||'—'}</td>
      <td><button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="markSettled('${c.id}')">✓ Settled</button></td>
    </tr>`;
  }).join('');
}

async function markSettled(id){
  const c=campaigns.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!c)return;
  c.status='settled';addAlert('info',`Settlement confirmed: ${c.address}`,`${f$(c.salePrice)} settled ${fD(today())}`);
  await syncDB();render();toast('Settlement confirmed','ok');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MARK SOLD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function openSoldModal(cid){
  soldCampId=cid;const c=campaigns.find(x=>x.id===cid);if(!c)return;
  document.getElementById('soldSub').textContent=c.address+', '+(c.suburb||'');
  document.getElementById('soldInfo').innerHTML=`<strong>${c.address}</strong>${c.suburb?', '+c.suburb:''} — ${c.process} closed ${fD(c.closeDate)}<br><span style="color:var(--mid)">${c.agent||'—'} · ${c.classification||'—'}</span>${c.notes?'<br><em style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.77rem">'+c.notes.substring(0,150)+'</em>':''}`;
  document.getElementById('s_price').value='';
  document.getElementById('s_solddate').value=today();
  document.getElementById('s_settle').value=addDays(today(),42);
  ['s_purchaser','s_vendor','s_netrent','s_yield','s_notes'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).value='');
  document.getElementById('s_days').value='42';
  openM('soldModal');
}

async function confirmSold(){
  const price=parseFloat(document.getElementById('s_price').value)||null;
  const soldDate=document.getElementById('s_solddate').value;
  const settleDate=document.getElementById('s_settle').value;
  if(!soldDate||!settleDate){toast('Sold date and settlement date required');return;}
  const c=campaigns.find(x=>x.id===soldCampId);if(!c)return;
  c.status='settlement';c.salePrice=price;c.soldDate=soldDate;c.settlementDate=settleDate;
  c.purchaser=document.getElementById('s_purchaser').value;
  const nr=document.getElementById('s_notes').value;
  if(nr)c.notes=(c.notes||'')+' | RESULT: '+nr;
  const netRent=parseFloat(document.getElementById('s_netrent').value)||null;
  const yld=price&&netRent?netRent/price:null;
  const ex=sales.find(s=>getAddr(s).toLowerCase()===c.address.toLowerCase()&&getSuburb(s).toLowerCase()===(c.suburb||'').toLowerCase());
  if(ex){ex.status='Sold';ex.price=price;ex.saleDate=soldDate;ex.settlementDate=settleDate;ex.purchaser=c.purchaser;ex.yld=yld;}
  else{sales.unshift({id:gId(),'Source Database':'Metro',source:'Metro',address:c.address,suburb:c.suburb||'',classification:c.classification||'',process:c.process||'',price,netRent,yld,saleDate:soldDate,settlementDate:settleDate,campaignClose:c.closeDate,agent:c.agent||'',purchaser:c.purchaser,status:'Sold',comments:c.notes||''});}
  addAlert('sold',`🎉 SOLD: ${c.address}${c.suburb?', '+c.suburb:''}`,`${f$(price)} · Settlement ${fD(settleDate)}`);
  await syncDB();render();closeM('soldModal');toast('Marked sold — settlement tracking started!','ok');
  showView('settlement',document.getElementById('nav-settlement'));
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// YIELD & RATE BENCHMARK
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function populateBenchmarkSuburbs(){
  const suburbs=[...new Set(getFilteredSales().map(s=>getSuburb(s)).filter(Boolean))].sort();
  const sel=document.getElementById('bk-suburb');
  if(sel)sel.innerHTML='<option value="">All Suburbs</option>'+suburbs.map(s=>`<option>${s}</option>`).join('');
}

function renderBenchmark(){
  const suburb=(document.getElementById('bk-suburb')||{value:''}).value;
  const cls=(document.getElementById('bk-class')||{value:''}).value;
  const yfrom=(document.getElementById('bk-yfrom')||{value:''}).value;
  const yto=(document.getElementById('bk-yto')||{value:''}).value;
  const minp=(parseFloat((document.getElementById('bk-minp')||{value:''}).value)||0)*1e6;
  const maxp=parseFloat((document.getElementById('bk-maxp')||{value:''}).value)?parseFloat(document.getElementById('bk-maxp').value)*1e6:Infinity;

  const lbl=document.getElementById('bench-db-label');
  if(lbl)lbl.textContent=activeDB==='all'?'(All Databases)':'('+activeDB+' only)';

  const fil=getFilteredSales().filter(s=>{
    const price=getPrice(s);if(!price||price<=0)return false;
    if(suburb&&getSuburb(s)!==suburb)return false;
    if(cls&&getCls(s)!==cls)return false;
    if(price<minp||price>maxp)return false;
    const sd=getSd(s)||'';
    if(yfrom&&sd&&sd.substring(0,4)<yfrom)return false;
    if(yto&&sd&&sd.substring(0,4)>yto)return false;
    return true;
  });

  const yields=fil.filter(s=>getYld(s)).map(s=>{const y=getYld(s);return y<1?y*100:parseFloat(y);});
  const rates=fil.filter(s=>getLR(s)>500).map(s=>getLR(s));
  const prices=fil.map(s=>getPrice(s));
  const haRates=fil.filter(s=>s['Land $ / Ha']>0).map(s=>s['Land $ / Ha']);

  const byY={},byYR={};
  fil.filter(s=>getYld(s)&&getSd(s)).forEach(s=>{const y=getSd(s).substring(0,4);if(!byY[y])byY[y]=[];byY[y].push(getYld(s)<1?getYld(s)*100:parseFloat(getYld(s)));});
  fil.filter(s=>getLR(s)>500&&getSd(s)).forEach(s=>{const y=getSd(s).substring(0,4);if(!byYR[y])byYR[y]=[];byYR[y].push(getLR(s));});
  const bySub={};
  fil.forEach(s=>{const sub=getSuburb(s);if(!bySub[sub])bySub[sub]={count:0,yields:[],rates:[],vol:0};bySub[sub].count++;bySub[sub].vol+=getPrice(s)||0;if(getYld(s))bySub[sub].yields.push(getYld(s)<1?getYld(s)*100:parseFloat(getYld(s)));if(getLR(s)>500)bySub[sub].rates.push(getLR(s));});
  const yrs=Object.keys(byY).sort();
  const maxY=Math.max(...Object.values(byY).map(v=>avg(v)||0),1);
  const maxR=Math.max(...Object.values(byYR).map(v=>avg(v)||0),1);
  const procMix=fil.reduce((a,s)=>{const p=s.process||s['Process']||'Unknown';a[p]=(a[p]||0)+1;return a;},{});
  const label=`${suburb||'All Suburbs'} · ${cls||'All Types'}${yfrom?(' · '+yfrom+(yto?'–'+yto:'+')):''} — ${fil.length} sales`;

  document.getElementById('benchContent').innerHTML=`
    <p style="font-size:.77rem;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.55rem">${label}</p>
    <div class="bench-grid">
      <div class="bench-card">
        <h3>📊 Yield Profile</h3>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Median Net Yield</span><span class="bsv red">${med(yields)?med(yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Average Net Yield</span><span class="bsv">${avg(yields)?avg(yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Range</span><span class="bsv">${yields.length?Math.min(...yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'} – ${yields.length?Math.max(...yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Sales with yield data</span><span class="bsv">${yields.length}</span></div>
        <div style="margin-top:.68rem;font-size:.6rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.42rem">Avg Yield by Year</div>
        ${yrs.map(y=>{const a=avg(byY[y]);return a?`<div class="br-row"><div class="br-lbl">${y}</div><div class="br-track"><div class="br-fill" style="width:${(a/maxY*100)}%"></div></div><div class="br-val">${a.toFixed(2)}% <span style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.6rem">(${byY[y].length})</span></div></div>`:''}).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="bench-card">
        <h3>💰 Price Analysis</h3>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Median Sale Price</span><span class="bsv red">${f$(med(prices))}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Average Sale Price</span><span class="bsv">${f$(avg(prices))}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Min / Max</span><span class="bsv">${f$(Math.min(...prices))} – ${f$(Math.max(...prices))}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Total Volume</span><span class="bsv">${f$(prices.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0))}</span></div>
        <div style="margin-top:.68rem;font-size:.6rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.42rem">Avg Land Rate by Year</div>
        ${yrs.map(y=>{const a=avg(byYR[y]);return a?`<div class="br-row"><div class="br-lbl">${y}</div><div class="br-track"><div class="br-fill" style="width:${(a/maxR*100)}%"></div></div><div class="br-val">${'$'+Math.round(a).toLocaleString()+'/m²'} <span style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.6rem">(${byYR[y].length})</span></div></div>`:''}).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="bench-card">
        <h3>📐 Land Rate Profile</h3>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Median Land Rate</span><span class="bsv red">${fM(med(rates))}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Average Land Rate</span><span class="bsv">${fM(avg(rates))}</span></div>
        <div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Sales with rate data</span><span class="bsv">${rates.length}</span></div>
        ${activeDB==='NSW Land'&&haRates.length?`<div class="bsr"><span class="bsl">Avg Land $/Ha</span><span class="bsv red">$${Math.round(avg(haRates)).toLocaleString()}/Ha</span></div>`:''}
        <div style="margin-top:.68rem;font-size:.6rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.42rem">Process Mix</div>
        ${Object.entries(procMix).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).map(([p,c])=>`<div class="br-row"><div class="br-lbl">${p}</div><div class="br-track"><div class="br-fill" style="width:${(c/fil.length*100)}%"></div></div><div class="br-val">${c} (${Math.round(c/fil.length*100)}%)</div></div>`).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="bench-card">
        <h3>📍 Suburb Breakdown</h3>
        <div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="ctable">
          <tr><th>Suburb</th><th>Sales</th><th>Avg Yield</th><th>Avg Rate/m²</th><th>Volume</th></tr>
          ${Object.entries(bySub).sort((a,b)=>b[1].count-a[1].count).slice(0,12).map(([sub,d])=>`
          <tr><td style="font-weight:500">${sub}</td><td>${d.count}</td>
          <td class="yv">${avg(d.yields)?avg(d.yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem">${avg(d.rates)?'$'+Math.round(avg(d.rates)).toLocaleString():'—'}</td>
          <td class="mono" style="font-size:.74rem">${f$(d.vol)}</td></tr>`).join('')}
        </table></div>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="bench-card" style="margin-top:.6rem">
      <h3>📋 Comparable Sales (${fil.length} records)</h3>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="ctable">
        <tr><th>Address</th><th>Suburb</th><th>Price</th><th>Date</th><th>Yield</th><th>Land $/m²</th><th>Land m²</th><th>Agent</th><th>Process</th></tr>
        ${fil.sort((a,b)=>(getSd(b)||'').localeCompare(getSd(a)||'')).slice(0,60).map(s=>`
        <tr onclick="openDetail('${s.id||s['ID']}')">
          <td style="font-weight:500;max-width:150px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap">${getAddr(s)}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem;color:var(--mid)">${getSuburb(s)}</td>
          <td class="mono">${f$(getPrice(s))}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem">${fD(getSd(s))}</td>
          <td class="yv">${yldD(getYld(s))}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem">${fM(getLR(s))}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem">${s.landArea||s['Land Area (m2)']?((s.landArea||s['Land Area (m2)'])+'m²'):'—'}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mid)">${getAgent(s)||'—'}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.7rem">${s.process||s['Process']||'—'}</td>
        </tr>`).join('')}
      </table></div>
    </div>`;
}

function clearBenchmark(){
  ['bk-suburb','bk-class','bk-yfrom','bk-yto'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
  ['bk-minp','bk-maxp'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
  renderBenchmark();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// AGENT LEAGUE TABLE — no recruit badges
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function renderAgents(){
  const reg=(document.getElementById('ag-region')||{value:''}).value;
  const cls=(document.getElementById('ag-class')||{value:''}).value;
  const yr=(document.getElementById('ag-year')||{value:''}).value;

  const fil=getFilteredSales().filter(s=>{
    if(!getAgent(s)&&!(s.coAgent||s['Co Agent']))return false;
    if(reg&&getRegion(s)!==reg)return false;
    if(cls&&getCls(s)!==cls)return false;
    if(yr&&(!getSd(s)||!getSd(s).startsWith(yr)))return false;
    return true;
  });

  const agMap={};
  const addAg=(name,s)=>{
    if(!name||!name.trim())return;
    const n=name.trim();
    if(!agMap[n])agMap[n]={name:n,count:0,vol:0,yields:[],rates:[],classes:{},suburbs:{},processes:{}};
    const a=agMap[n];a.count++;const price=getPrice(s);if(price)a.vol+=price;
    const y=getYld(s);if(y)a.yields.push(y<1?y*100:parseFloat(y));
    const lr=getLR(s);if(lr>500)a.rates.push(lr);
    const c=getCls(s);if(c)a.classes[c]=(a.classes[c]||0)+1;
    const sub=getSuburb(s);if(sub)a.suburbs[sub]=(a.suburbs[sub]||0)+1;
    const pr=s.process||s['Process'];if(pr)a.processes[pr]=(a.processes[pr]||0)+1;
  };
  fil.forEach(s=>{addAg(getAgent(s),s);const co=s.coAgent||s['Co Agent'];if(co)addAg(co,s);});
  const agents=Object.values(agMap).sort((a,b)=>b.vol-a.vol);
  const topKey=obj=>Object.entries(obj).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1])[0]?.[0]||'—';
  document.getElementById('ag-cnt').textContent=`${agents.length} agents · ${fil.length} sales`;

  document.getElementById('leagueWrap').innerHTML=`
    <div class="league-grid">
      <div class="league-card">
        <h3>🏆 By Total Volume</h3>
        ${agents.slice(0,15).map((a,i)=>{
          const r=i===0?'gold':i===1?'silver':i===2?'bronze':'';
          return `<div class="ag-row">
            <div class="ag-rank ${r}">#${i+1}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div class="ag-name">${a.name}</div>
              <div style="font-size:.64rem;color:var(--mid)">${a.count} sales · ${topKey(a.suburbs)} · ${topKey(a.classes)}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="ag-vol">${f$(a.vol)}</div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
      <div class="league-card">
        <h3>📊 By Transaction Count</h3>
        ${[...agents].sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count).slice(0,15).map((a,i)=>{
          const r=i===0?'gold':i===1?'silver':i===2?'bronze':'';
          return `<div class="ag-row">
            <div class="ag-rank ${r}">#${i+1}</div>
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0">
              <div class="ag-name">${a.name}</div>
              <div style="font-size:.64rem;color:var(--mid)">${topKey(a.suburbs)} · Avg yield ${avg(a.yields)?avg(a.yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="ag-vol">${a.count} sales</div>
          </div>`;
        }).join('')}
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="league-card" style="margin-top:.6rem">
      <h3>📋 Full Agent Intelligence — ${agents.length} agents</h3>
      <div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="ctable">
        <tr><th>#</th><th>Agent</th><th>Sales</th><th>Total Volume</th><th>Avg Price</th><th>Avg Yield</th><th>Avg Rate/m²</th><th>Top Suburb</th><th>Specialty</th><th>Top Process</th></tr>
        ${agents.map((a,i)=>`<tr>
          <td style="color:var(--mid);font-size:.72rem">${i+1}</td>
          <td style="font-weight:600;font-size:.78rem">${a.name}</td>
          <td>${a.count}</td>
          <td class="mono" style="font-size:.77rem">${f$(a.vol)}</td>
          <td class="mono" style="font-size:.77rem">${a.count?f$(a.vol/a.count):'—'}</td>
          <td class="yv">${avg(a.yields)?avg(a.yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.74rem">${avg(a.rates)?'$'+Math.round(avg(a.rates)).toLocaleString():'—'}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.73rem">${topKey(a.suburbs)}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.71rem;color:var(--mid)">${topKey(a.classes)}</td>
          <td style="font-size:.71rem">${topKey(a.processes)}</td>
        </tr>`).join('')}
      </table></div>
    </div>`;
}
