  'Tamarama':[-33.9010,151.2680],'Bronte':[-33.9040,151.2670],'Clovelly':[-33.9148,151.2664],
  'Coogee':[-33.9200,151.2588],'Maroubra':[-33.9478,151.2455],'Randwick':[-33.9145,151.2370],
  'Manly':[-33.7969,151.2875],'Dee Why':[-33.7488,151.2876],'Freshwater':[-33.7750,151.2877],
  'Newport':[-33.6533,151.3278],'Mona Vale':[-33.6774,151.3028],
  'Waterloo':[-33.9012,151.2075],'Rosebery':[-33.9160,151.1980],
  'Auburn':[-33.8495,151.0326],'Granville':[-33.8336,151.0134],
  'Merrylands':[-33.8353,150.9906],'Northmead':[-33.7934,150.9860],
  'Norwest':[-33.7295,150.9715],'Castle Hill':[-33.7293,151.0028],
  'Campsie':[-33.9083,151.1037],'Woollahra':[-33.8907,151.2536],
  'Waverley':[-33.9076,151.2524],'Bondi Junction':[-33.8914,151.2480],
  'Rushcutters Bay':[-33.8731,151.2310],'Elizabeth Bay':[-33.8721,151.2280],
};

function getCoords(s){
  const sub=(getSuburb(s)||'').trim();
  if(SUBURB_COORDS[sub])return SUBURB_COORDS[sub];
  for(const[k,v]of Object.entries(SUBURB_COORDS))if(sub.toLowerCase().includes(k.toLowerCase()))return v;
  return null;
}

function initMap(){
  const cls=(document.getElementById('map-class')||{value:''}).value;
  const st=(document.getElementById('map-status')||{value:''}).value;
  const yr=(document.getElementById('map-year')||{value:''}).value;
  if(!mapInst){mapInst=L.map('mapEl').setView([-33.87,151.21],11);L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png',{attribution:'© OpenStreetMap'}).addTo(mapInst);}
  mapMarkers.forEach(m=>m.remove());mapMarkers=[];
  const fil=getFilteredSales().filter(s=>{
    if(cls&&getCls(s)!==cls)return false;
    if(st&&getStatus(s)!==st)return false;
    if(yr&&(!getSd(s)||!getSd(s).startsWith(yr)))return false;
    return true;
  });
  let shown=0;
  fil.forEach(s=>{
    const coords=getCoords(s);if(!coords)return;
    const src=getSrc(s);
    const color=getStatus(s)==='Sold'?'#2E7D32':getStatus(s)==='Active'?'#E1251B':'#6D6E71';
    const srcColor=src==='NSW Land'?'#2E7D32':src==='Premium Investment'?'#E65100':'#1565C0';
    const m=L.circleMarker(coords,{radius:7,fillColor:color,color:'#fff',weight:1.5,opacity:1,fillOpacity:0.85}).addTo(mapInst);
    m.bindPopup(`<div style="min-width:190px;font-family:'Barlow',sans-serif">
      <div style="font-weight:700;font-size:.82rem;border-bottom:2px solid #E1251B;padding-bottom:.26rem;margin-bottom:.35rem">${getAddr(s)}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.7rem;color:${srcColor};font-weight:700;margin-bottom:.16rem">${src}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.74rem;color:#6D6E71;margin-bottom:.26rem">${getSuburb(s)} · ${getCls(s)||'—'}</div>
      <div style="font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1rem;font-weight:700;color:#E1251B">${f$(getPrice(s))}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.71rem;margin-top:.2rem">Yield: ${yldD(getYld(s))} · ${fM(getLR(s))}</div>
      <div style="font-size:.69rem;color:#6D6E71;margin-top:.16rem">${fD(getSd(s))} · ${getAgent(s)||'—'}</div>
    </div>`);
    mapMarkers.push(m);shown++;
  });
  document.getElementById('map-cnt').textContent=shown+' properties mapped';
}
function renderMap(){if(mapInst)initMap();}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ANALYTICS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function renderAnalytics(){
  const anaSales=getFilteredSales();
  const wp=anaSales.filter(s=>getPrice(s)>0);const vol=wp.reduce((a,s)=>a+getPrice(s),0);
  const yields=anaSales.filter(s=>getYld(s)).map(s=>{const y=getYld(s);return y<1?y*100:parseFloat(y);});
  const rates=anaSales.filter(s=>getLR(s)>500).map(s=>getLR(s));
  const byType={},byYear={},byProc={},byReg={};
  anaSales.forEach(s=>{
    const cls=getCls(s);if(cls)byType[cls]=(byType[cls]||0)+1;
    const yr=getSd(s)?.substring(0,4);const price=getPrice(s)||0;if(yr)byYear[yr]=(byYear[yr]||0)+price;
    const pr=s.process||s['Process'];if(pr)byProc[pr]=(byProc[pr]||0)+1;
    const reg=getRegion(s);if(reg)byReg[reg]=(byReg[reg]||0)+price;
  });
  const bar=(data,maxV,fmt=v=>v)=>Object.entries(data).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).slice(0,8).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="br-row"><div class="br-lbl" title="${k}">${k}</div><div class="br-track"><div class="br-fill" style="width:${Math.min(100,(v/maxV)*100)}%"></div></div><div class="br-val">${fmt(v)}</div></div>`).join('');
  const yB={'<3%':0,'3–4%':0,'4–5%':0,'5–6%':0,'6–7%':0,'>7%':0};
  yields.forEach(y=>{if(y<3)yB['<3%']++;else if(y<4)yB['3–4%']++;else if(y<5)yB['4–5%']++;else if(y<6)yB['5–6%']++;else if(y<7)yB['6–7%']++;else yB['>7%']++;});
  document.getElementById('analyticsWrap').innerHTML=`
    <div class="ac full"><h3>Portfolio Summary</h3><div class="kpi-grid">
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${anaSales.length}</div><div class="kpib-l">Total Records</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${f$(vol)}</div><div class="kpib-l">Total Volume</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${avg(yields)?avg(yields).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'}</div><div class="kpib-l">Avg Yield</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${avg(rates)?'$'+Math.round(avg(rates)).toLocaleString()+'/m²':'—'}</div><div class="kpib-l">Avg Land Rate</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${wp.length?f$(vol/wp.length):'—'}</div><div class="kpib-l">Avg Sale Price</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${anaSales.filter(s=>getStatus(s)==='Sold').length}</div><div class="kpib-l">Confirmed Sold</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0).length}</div><div class="kpib-l">Active Campaigns</div></div>
      <div class="kpib"><div class="kpib-v">${campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement').length}</div><div class="kpib-l">In Settlement</div></div>
    </div></div>
    <div class="ac"><h3>Sales by Asset Class</h3>${bar(byType,Math.max(...Object.values(byType),1),v=>v+' sales')}</div>
    <div class="ac"><h3>Volume by Year</h3>${bar(byYear,Math.max(...Object.values(byYear),1),v=>f$(v))}</div>
    <div class="ac"><h3>Sales by Process</h3>${bar(byProc,Math.max(...Object.values(byProc),1),v=>v+' sales')}</div>
    <div class="ac"><h3>Volume by Region</h3>${bar(byReg,Math.max(...Object.values(byReg),1),v=>f$(v))}</div>
    <div class="ac"><h3>Yield Distribution</h3>${Object.entries(yB).map(([k,v])=>`<div class="br-row"><div class="br-lbl">${k}</div><div class="br-track"><div class="br-fill" style="width:${Math.max((v/Math.max(...Object.values(yB),1))*100,2)}%"></div></div><div class="br-val">${v} sales</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// INTELLIGENCE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function addAlert(type,title,meta_){
  alerts.unshift({id:gId(),type,title,meta:meta_,read:false,ts:new Date().toISOString()});
  if(alerts.length>100)alerts=alerts.slice(0,100);
}

function renderIntelligence(){
  const wrap=document.getElementById('alertWrap');if(!wrap)return;
  if(alerts.length===0){
    wrap.innerHTML=`<div class="run-check-box" onclick="runWeeklyCheck()">
      <div style="font-size:1.8rem">⚡</div>
      <strong style="font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:.9rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em">Run Weekly Intelligence Check</strong>
      <p style="font-size:.77rem">Checks RealCommercial &amp; CommercialRealEstate for all active campaigns</p>
    </div>`;return;
  }
  const imap={sold:'🔴',new:'🟢',warn:'🟡',info:'🔵'};
  wrap.innerHTML=alerts.map(a=>`<div class="alert-card ${a.type}" onclick="markRead('${a.id}')" style="cursor:pointer">
    <div style="font-size:.95rem;flex-shrink:0;margin-top:.08rem">${imap[a.type]||'ℹ'}</div>
    <div style="flex:1">
      <div class="alert-title">${a.title}${!a.read?' <span style="background:var(--red);color:white;border-radius:10px;padding:.06rem .35rem;font-size:.56rem;font-weight:700;vertical-align:middle">NEW</span>':''}</div>
      <div class="alert-meta">${a.meta} · ${new Date(a.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</div>
    </div>
  </div>`).join('');
}

function markRead(id){const a=alerts.find(x=>x.id===id);if(a)a.read=true;updateBadges();}
function clearAlerts(){alerts=[];renderIntelligence();updateBadges();}

async function runWeeklyCheck(){
  showView('intelligence',document.getElementById('nav-intelligence'));
  addAlert('info','⚡ Intelligence check running — come back to Claude to run this properly','Use Claude.ai to run the weekly intelligence check with full web search capabilities');
  renderIntelligence();updateBadges();
  meta.lastCheck=new Date().toISOString();updateStats();
  toast('Open Claude.ai to run the full intelligence check','info');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// MARKET PULSE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function generatePulse(){
  const wk=new Date();wk.setDate(wk.getDate()-7);const wkStr=wk.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const recent=sales.filter(s=>getPrice(s)&&getSd(s)&&getSd(s)>=wkStr).sort((a,b)=>getPrice(b)-getPrice(a)).slice(0,8);
  const upcoming=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0&&daysTo(c.closeDate)<=21).sort((a,b)=>daysTo(a.closeDate)-daysTo(b.closeDate));
  const inSettle=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='settlement').slice(0,5);
  const stale=sales.filter(isStale).length;
  const yields2025=sales.filter(s=>getYld(s)&&getSd(s)?.startsWith('2025')).map(s=>{const y=getYld(s);return y<1?y*100:parseFloat(y);});
  const totalVol=sales.filter(s=>getPrice(s)).reduce((a,s)=>a+getPrice(s),0);
  const acCount=campaigns.filter(c=>c.status==='active'&&daysTo(c.closeDate)>=0).length;
  const dateStr=new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'long',year:'numeric'});

  const html=`<div style="font-family:Arial,sans-serif;max-width:660px;margin:0 auto;background:#f5f5f5;padding:20px">
  <div style="background:#231F20;padding:18px 22px;border-bottom:3px solid #E1251B">
    <div style="font-size:15px;font-weight:700;color:white;letter-spacing:.06em;text-transform:uppercase">Cushman &amp; Wakefield</div>
    <div style="font-size:10px;color:#D1D3D4;letter-spacing:.14em;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:2px">NSW Sales Intelligence — Market Pulse</div>
    <div style="font-size:11px;color:rgba(255,255,255,.6);margin-top:4px">${dateStr}</div>
  </div>
  <div style="background:white;border:1px solid #E0E0E0;margin-top:12px">
    <div style="background:#E1251B;padding:10px 16px;color:white;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em">📊 Database Summary</div>
    <div style="display:flex">
      ${[['Total Records',sales.length],['Total Volume',f$(totalVol)],['Avg Yield 2025',avg(yields2025)?avg(yields2025).toFixed(2)+'%':'—'],['Active Campaigns',acCount]].map(([l,v])=>`<div style="flex:1;padding:12px;text-align:center;border-right:1px solid #f0f0f0"><div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:700;color:#231F20">${v}</div><div style="font-size:9px;color:#6D6E71;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;margin-top:2px">${l}</div></div>`).join('')}
    </div>
  </div>
  ${recent.length?`<div style="background:white;border:1px solid #E0E0E0;margin-top:10px">
    <div style="background:#231F20;padding:10px 16px;color:white;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em">🔴 Recent Sales (Last 7 Days)</div>
    ${recent.map(s=>`<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #f5f5f5">
      <div><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:12px">${getAddr(s)}, ${getSuburb(s)}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">${getCls(s)||'—'} · ${getAgent(s)||'—'} · ${s.process||s['Process']||'—'}</div></div>
      <div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-weight:700;color:#E1251B;font-size:13px">${f$(getPrice(s))}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">${getYld(s)?yldD(getYld(s))+' yield':''}</div></div>
    </div>`).join('')}
  </div>`:''}
  ${upcoming.length?`<div style="background:white;border:1px solid #E0E0E0;margin-top:10px">
    <div style="background:#B8860B;padding:10px 16px;color:white;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em">⏰ Campaigns Closing Next 21 Days</div>
    ${upcoming.map(c=>{const d=daysTo(c.closeDate);return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #f5f5f5">
      <div><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:12px">${c.address}${c.suburb?', '+c.suburb:''}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">${c.classification||'—'} · ${c.agent||'—'} · ${c.process}</div></div>
      <div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-weight:700;color:${d<=7?'#E1251B':'#B8860B'};font-size:13px">${d===0?'TODAY!':d+'d'}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">${fD(c.closeDate)}</div></div>
    </div>`;}).join('')}
  </div>`:''}
  ${inSettle.length?`<div style="background:white;border:1px solid #E0E0E0;margin-top:10px">
    <div style="background:#1565C0;padding:10px 16px;color:white;font-weight:700;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em">🏦 In Settlement</div>
    ${inSettle.map(c=>{const d=daysTo(c.settlementDate);return `<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:10px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid #f5f5f5">
      <div><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:12px">${c.address}${c.suburb?', '+c.suburb:''}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">Purchaser: ${c.purchaser||'Undisclosed'}</div></div>
      <div style="text-align:right"><div style="font-weight:700;color:#1565C0;font-size:13px">${f$(c.salePrice)}</div><div style="font-size:10px;color:#6D6E71">${d!==null&&d>=0?d+'d to settle':'Settled'}</div></div>
    </div>`;}).join('')}
  </div>`:''}
  ${stale>0?`<div style="background:#FDF5E8;border:1px solid #F0C080;margin-top:10px;padding:12px 16px"><span style="font-weight:700;color:#B8860B">⚠ ${stale} Stale Records</span> <span style="font-size:11px;color:#6D6E71">— Active status but campaign close date 90+ days ago. Please review.</span></div>`:''}
  <div style="text-align:center;padding:12px;font-size:10px;color:#aaa;margin-top:10px">Generated by CW NSW Sales Intelligence · ${dateStr}</div>
</div>`;

  document.getElementById('pulseWrap').innerHTML=`<div style="background:var(--white);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden">${html}</div>`;
  window._pulseHTML=html;
}

function copyPulseHTML(){navigator.clipboard.writeText(window._pulseHTML||'').then(()=>toast('HTML copied','ok')).catch(()=>toast('Copy failed'));}
function openGmailPulse(){
  const sub=encodeURIComponent('CW NSW Sales Intelligence — Market Pulse '+new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'}));
  window.open(`https://mail.google.com/mail/?view=cm&fs=1&su=${sub}`,'_blank');
  copyPulseHTML();toast('Gmail opened — paste copied HTML into body','info');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// EXPORT TO EXCEL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function exportExcel(){
  const srch=(document.getElementById('srch')||{value:''}).value.toLowerCase();
  const fC=(document.getElementById('fClass')||{value:''}).value;
  const fS=(document.getElementById('fStatus')||{value:''}).value;
  const fP=(document.getElementById('fProcess')||{value:''}).value;
  const fY=(document.getElementById('fYear')||{value:''}).value;
  const fR=(document.getElementById('fRegion')||{value:''}).value;

  const fil=getFilteredSales().filter(s=>{
    const txt=[getAddr(s),getSuburb(s),getAgent(s),getCls(s),getRegion(s)].join(' ').toLowerCase();
    if(srch&&!txt.includes(srch))return false;
    if(fC&&getCls(s)!==fC)return false;
    if(fS==='stale')return isStale(s);
    if(fS&&getStatus(s)!==fS)return false;
    if(fP&&(s.process||s['Process'])!==fP)return false;
    if(fY&&(!getSd(s)||!getSd(s).startsWith(fY)))return false;
    if(fR&&getRegion(s)!==fR)return false;
    return true;
  });

  if(!fil.length){toast('No records to export');return;}

  const COLS=['ID','Source Database','Address','Suburb','Region','Classification','Status',
    'Sale Price','Price Guidance','Net Rent','Yield','Sale Date','Settlement Date',
    'Process','Campaign Close Date','RC Status','Land Area (m2)','Land $ / m2',
    'Floor Area (m2)','WALE','Land Area (Ha)','Land $ / Ha','Primary Zoning','FSR',
    'Height (m)','Development Stage','Constraints','Agent','Co Agent','Vendor','Purchaser','Comments'];

  const rows=fil.map(s=>{
    const row={};
    COLS.forEach(col=>{
      const v=gsRecToRow(s,GS_SALES_COLS)[GS_SALES_COLS.indexOf(col)]||'';
      row[col]=v;
    });
    return row;
  });

  const wb=XLSX.utils.book_new();
  const ws=XLSX.utils.json_to_sheet(rows,{header:COLS});
  ws['!cols']=COLS.map(c=>({wch:c==='Address'?28:c==='Comments'?32:c.includes('Date')?13:12}));
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws,'Sales Export');

  // Summary sheet
  const bySrc={};
  fil.forEach(s=>{const src=getSrc(s);if(!bySrc[src])bySrc[src]={count:0,vol:0};bySrc[src].count++;const p=getPrice(s);if(p)bySrc[src].vol+=p;});
  const sumData=[
    ['CW NSW Sales Intelligence — Export','',''],
    ['Generated',new Date().toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'}),''],
    ['Database Filter',activeDB==='all'?'All Databases':activeDB,''],
    ['Records Exported',fil.length,''],
    ['','',''],
    ['Source Database','Records','Total Volume'],
    ...Object.entries(bySrc).map(([src,d])=>[src,d.count,d.vol?'$'+Math.round(d.vol).toLocaleString():'—']),
  ];
  const ws2=XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(sumData);
  ws2['!cols']=[{wch:28},{wch:18},{wch:18}];
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb,ws2,'Summary');

  const dateStr=new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
  const dbStr=activeDB==='all'?'All':activeDB.replace(/ /g,'_');
  XLSX.writeFile(wb,`CW_NSW_Sales_${dbStr}_${dateStr}.xlsx`);
  toast('✓ Exported '+fil.length+' records','ok');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PDF BATCH QUEUE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const qDrop=document.getElementById('qDrop');
qDrop.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();qDrop.classList.add('over');});
qDrop.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>qDrop.classList.remove('over'));
qDrop.addEventListener('drop',e=>{e.preventDefault();qDrop.classList.remove('over');addToQueue([...e.dataTransfer.files].filter(f=>f.type==='application/pdf'));});
function handleQueueFiles(e){addToQueue([...e.target.files]);}
function addToQueue(files){files.forEach(f=>queueFiles.push({file:f,status:'pending',result:null}));renderQueue();updateBadges();}
function renderQueue(){
  const list=document.getElementById('qList');
  list.style.display=queueFiles.length?'':'none';
  document.getElementById('processQBtn').style.display=queueFiles.filter(q=>q.status==='pending').length?'':'none';
  list.innerHTML=queueFiles.map((q,i)=>`<div class="qi">
    <div style="font-size:1rem">📄</div>
    <div class="qi-name">${q.file.name}</div>
    <span class="qi-badge ${q.status==='pending'?'qi-pending':q.status==='processing'?'qi-proc':q.status==='done'?'qi-done':'qi-err'}">${q.status==='pending'?'Pending':q.status==='processing'?'Processing…':q.status==='done'?'Done ✓':'Error'}</span>
    ${q.result?`<button class="btn btn-green btn-sm" onclick="useQueueResult(${i})">Add to DB</button>`:''}
    ${q.status==='pending'?`<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="removeFromQueue(${i})">×</button>`:''}
  </div>`).join('');
}
function removeFromQueue(i){queueFiles.splice(i,1);renderQueue();updateBadges();}
function clearQueue(){queueFiles=[];renderQueue();updateBadges();}
async function processQueue(){
  const btn=document.getElementById('processQBtn');btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='Processing…';
  for(let i=0;i<queueFiles.length;i++){
    const q=queueFiles[i];if(q.status!=='pending')continue;
    q.status='processing';renderQueue();
    try{
      const b64=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>res(r.result.split(',')[1]);r.onerror=rej;r.readAsDataURL(q.file);});
      const resp=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:1000,messages:[{role:'user',content:[{type:'document',source:{type:'base64',media_type:'application/pdf',data:b64}},{type:'text',text:'Extract all commercial property details. Return ONLY valid JSON (no markdown): {"address":null,"suburb":null,"classification":null,"zoning":null,"landArea":null,"floorArea":null,"fsr":null,"height":null,"price":null,"saleDate":null,"campaignClose":null,"netRent":null,"outgoings":null,"yld":null,"wale":null,"vendor":null,"purchaser":null,"agent":null,"coAgent":null,"process":null,"status":"Active","configuration":null,"constraints":null,"devStage":null,"comments":null,"source":"Metro"}'}]}]})});
      const d=await resp.json();const txt=d.content?.[0]?.text||'{}';
      q.result=JSON.parse(txt.replace(/```json|```/g,'').trim());
      if(q.result.price&&q.result.landArea)q.result.landRate=Math.round(q.result.price/q.result.landArea);
      if(q.result.price&&q.result.netRent)q.result.yld=q.result.netRent/q.result.price;
      q.status='done';
    }catch(e){q.status='error';q.error=e.message;}
    renderQueue();await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,500));
  }
  btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='▶ Process All';
  toast(`Processed — ${queueFiles.filter(q=>q.status==='done').length}/${queueFiles.length} successful`,'ok');
}
async function useQueueResult(i){
  const q=queueFiles[i];if(!q.result)return;
  q.result.id=gId();q.result.created=new Date().toISOString();
  if(!q.result.status)q.result.status='Active';
  if(!q.result['Source Database'])q.result['Source Database']=q.result.source||'Metro';
  sales.unshift(q.result);await syncDB();renderDB();updateStats();
  toast('Added: '+q.result.address,'ok');
  queueFiles.splice(i,1);renderQueue();updateBadges();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// BULK IMPORT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
const importDrop=document.getElementById('importDrop');
importDrop.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();importDrop.classList.add('over');});
importDrop.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>importDrop.classList.remove('over'));
importDrop.addEventListener('drop',e=>{e.preventDefault();importDrop.classList.remove('over');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f)processImportFile(f);});
function handleImportFile(e){const f=e.target.files[0];if(f)processImportFile(f);}
async function processImportFile(file){
  const text=await file.text();
  const lines=text.split(/\r?\n/).filter(Boolean);
  if(lines.length<2){toast('File appears empty');return;}
  importHeaders=lines[0].split(',').map(h=>h.replace(/"/g,'').trim());
  importRows=lines.slice(1).map(l=>{
    const vals=[];let cur='',inQ=false;
    for(const ch of l){if(ch==='"')inQ=!inQ;else if(ch===','&&!inQ){vals.push(cur.trim());cur='';}else cur+=ch;}
    vals.push(cur.trim());
    const obj={};importHeaders.forEach((h,i)=>obj[h]=vals[i]||'');return obj;
  }).filter(r=>Object.values(r).some(v=>v));
  showColMapModal();
}
function showColMapModal(){
  const DB_FIELDS=['address','suburb','region','classification','price','saleDate','netRent','yld','landArea','landRate','floorArea','process','campaignClose','agent','coAgent','vendor','purchaser','zoning','fsr','height','wale','configuration','comments','status','source'];
  document.getElementById('colMapSub').textContent=`${importRows.length} rows · ${importHeaders.length} columns`;
  document.getElementById('colMapBody').innerHTML=`<div class="col-map-grid">${importHeaders.map(h=>{
    const best=DB_FIELDS.find(f=>f.toLowerCase()===h.toLowerCase()||h.toLowerCase().includes(f.toLowerCase()))||'';
    return `<div class="cm-item"><label>${h}</label><select id="col-${h.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'_')}"><option value="">— Skip —</option>${DB_FIELDS.map(f=>`<option value="${f}"${f===best?' selected':''}>${f}</option>`).join('')}</select></div>`;
  }).join('')}</div>
  <div style="padding:.85rem;background:var(--pale);border-top:1px solid var(--border)">
    <div style="font-size:.73rem;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.38rem">Preview (first 5 rows)</div>
    <div style="overflow-x:auto"><table class="import-preview-table"><thead><tr>${importHeaders.map(h=>`<th>${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr></thead>
    <tbody>${importRows.slice(0,5).map(r=>`<tr>${importHeaders.map(h=>`<td>${r[h]||''}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>
  </div>`;
  openM('colMapModal');
}
async function commitImport(){
  const colMap={};
  importHeaders.forEach(h=>{const sel=document.getElementById('col-'+h.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/gi,'_'));if(sel&&sel.value)colMap[h]=sel.value;});
  let added=0,dupes=0;
  importRows.forEach(row=>{
    const rec={id:gId(),'Source Database':'Metro',source:'Metro',created:new Date().toISOString(),status:'Active'};
    Object.entries(colMap).forEach(([col,field])=>{
      let val=row[col];if(!val||val==='')return;
      const numFields=['price','netRent','yld','landArea','landRate','floorArea','fsr','height','wale'];
      if(numFields.includes(field))val=parseFloat(val.replace(/[$,]/g,''))||null;
      rec[field]=val;
    });
    if(!rec.address)return;
    const isDup=sales.some(s=>getAddr(s).toLowerCase()===rec.address.toLowerCase()&&getSuburb(s).toLowerCase()===(rec.suburb||'').toLowerCase());
    if(isDup){dupes++;return;}
    if(rec.price&&rec.landArea)rec.landRate=Math.round(rec.price/rec.landArea);
    if(rec.price&&rec.netRent&&!rec.yld)rec.yld=rec.netRent/rec.price;
    sales.unshift(rec);added++;
  });
  await syncDB();render();closeM('colMapModal');
  toast(`Imported ${added} records${dupes?' · '+dupes+' dupes skipped':''}`,'ok');
  runDupCheck(true);
}
function downloadTemplate(){
  const hdrs='Address,Suburb,Region,Classification,Price,Sale Date,Net Rent,Yield,Land Area m2,Land Rate,Floor Area m2,Process,Campaign Close Date,Agent,Co Agent,Vendor,Purchaser,Zoning,FSR,Height m,WALE,Configuration,Comments,Status,Source';
  const ex='96 Campbell Parade,Bondi Beach,Eastern Suburbs,Retail / Mixed Use,28500000,2026-04-21,518042,0.0182,430,66279,706.8,Auction,2026-04-21,Cushman & Wakefield,,,,E1,3,15,,Ground Floor Retail,,Active,Metro';
  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href='data:text/csv;charset=utf-8,'+encodeURIComponent(hdrs+'\n'+ex);a.download='CW_Import_Template.csv';a.click();
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// DUPLICATE DETECTOR
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function levenshtein(a,b){
  const m=a.length,n=b.length,d=Array.from({length:m+1},(_,i)=>Array.from({length:n+1},(_,j)=>i===0?j:j===0?i:0));
  for(let i=1;i<=m;i++)for(let j=1;j<=n;j++)d[i][j]=a[i-1]===b[j-1]?d[i-1][j-1]:1+Math.min(d[i-1][j],d[i][j-1],d[i-1][j-1]);
  return d[m][n];
}
function runDupCheck(silent=false){
  const groups=[];const seen=new Set();
  sales.forEach((s,i)=>{
    if(seen.has(i))return;
    const matches=[s];
    sales.forEach((t,j)=>{
      if(i===j||seen.has(j))return;
      const na=(getAddr(s)||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      const nb=(getAddr(t)||'').toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,'');
      const addrSim=na&&nb&&(na===nb||levenshtein(na,nb)<4);
      const subMatch=getSuburb(s)&&getSuburb(t)&&getSuburb(s).toLowerCase()===getSuburb(t).toLowerCase();
      const priceMatch=getPrice(s)&&getPrice(t)&&Math.abs(getPrice(s)-getPrice(t))<5000;
      if((addrSim&&subMatch)||(addrSim&&priceMatch)||(subMatch&&priceMatch&&getSd(s)&&getSd(t)&&getSd(s).substring(0,7)===getSd(t).substring(0,7))){matches.push(t);seen.add(j);}
    });
    if(matches.length>1){groups.push(matches);seen.add(i);}
  });
  const nb=document.getElementById('nb-dups');nb.textContent=groups.length;nb.style.display=groups.length?'':'none';
  if(!silent){
    if(groups.length===0){
      document.getElementById('dupWrap').innerHTML=`<div class="run-check-box"><div style="font-size:1.8rem">✅</div><strong>No duplicates detected</strong><p style="font-size:.78rem">All ${sales.length} records appear unique</p></div>`;
    }else{
      document.getElementById('dupWrap').innerHTML=groups.map(g=>`<div class="dup-group">
        <div class="dup-ghdr"><h3>Possible duplicate — ${g.length} records</h3>
        <div style="display:flex;gap:.32rem">${g.slice(1).map(s=>`<button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color:var(--red);border-color:var(--red)" onclick="deleteSaleById('${s.id||s['ID']}')">Delete</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
        ${g.map(s=>`<div class="dup-row"><div style="flex:1"><div style="font-weight:600;font-size:.79rem">${getAddr(s)}, ${getSuburb(s)}</div><div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--mid)">${getCls(s)||'—'} · ${fD(getSd(s))} · ${f$(getPrice(s))} · ${getAgent(s)||'—'}</div></div><span class="badge b-${getStatus(s).toLowerCase()}">${getStatus(s)}</span></div>`).join('')}
      </div>`).join('');
      toast(`${groups.length} duplicate group${groups.length>1?'s':''} found`,'info');
    }
  }
}
async function deleteSaleById(id){
  if(!confirm('Delete this record?'))return;
  sales=sales.filter(s=>(s.id||s['ID'])!==id);await syncDB();render();runDupCheck(false);toast('Deleted','ok');
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PROPERTY NOTES
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function addNote(saleId){
  const ta=document.getElementById('note-input-'+saleId);if(!ta||!ta.value.trim())return;
  if(!notes[saleId])notes[saleId]=[];
  notes[saleId].unshift({ts:new Date().toISOString(),text:ta.value.trim()});
  ta.value='';renderNotes(saleId);renderDB();toast('Note added','ok');
}
function renderNotes(saleId){
  const container=document.getElementById('notes-section-'+saleId);if(!container)return;
  const sn=notes[saleId]||[];
  container.innerHTML=`<div class="atitle">📝 Activity Log (${sn.length})</div>
    <div class="notes-log">${sn.length?sn.map(n=>`<div class="note-item"><div class="note-ts">${new Date(n.ts).toLocaleDateString('en-AU',{day:'2-digit',month:'short',year:'numeric'})} ${new Date(n.ts).toLocaleTimeString('en-AU',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</div><div class="note-text">${n.text}</div></div>`).join(''):'<p style="font-size:.77rem;color:var(--mid)">No notes yet</p>'}</div>
    <div class="note-row"><textarea id="note-input-${saleId}" placeholder="Add a note, update, or observation…"></textarea><button class="btn btn-red btn-sm" onclick="addNote('${saleId}')">Add</button></div>`;
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ADD / EDIT / DETAIL MODALS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function openAddModal(){
  editId=null;pdfData=null;
  document.getElementById('addTitle').textContent='Add New Sale';
  clearForm();document.getElementById('f_date').value=today();document.getElementById('f_status').value='Active';
  openM('addModal');
}
function clearForm(){
  ['f_address','f_suburb','f_land','f_floor','f_zoning','f_fsr','f_height','f_config','f_constraints',
   'f_price','f_priceguide','f_date','f_netrent','f_outgoings','f_yield','f_landrate',
   'f_closedate','f_settlementdate','f_wale','f_vendor','f_purchaser','f_agent','f_coagent','f_comments']
   .forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
  ['f_region','f_source','f_class','f_process','f_devstage'].forEach(id=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(el)el.value='';});
}
function fillForm(d){
  const m={f_address:'address',f_suburb:'suburb',f_region:'region',f_source:'source',
    f_class:'classification',f_land:'landArea',f_floor:'floorArea',f_zoning:'zoning',
    f_fsr:'fsr',f_height:'height',f_config:'configuration',f_constraints:'constraints',
    f_devstage:'devStage',f_price:'price',f_priceguide:'priceGuide',f_date:'saleDate',
    f_netrent:'netRent',f_outgoings:'outgoings',f_closedate:'campaignClose',
    f_settlementdate:'settlementDate',f_wale:'wale',f_vendor:'vendor',
    f_purchaser:'purchaser',f_agent:'agent',f_coagent:'coAgent',f_comments:'comments',
    f_status:'status',f_process:'process'};
  // Try both camelCase and flat field names
  const get=(key)=>{
    const aliases={address:['address','Address'],suburb:['suburb','Suburb'],
      region:['region','Region'],classification:['classification','Classification'],
      source:['source','Source Database'],price:['price','Sale Price'],
      priceGuide:['priceGuide','Price Guidance'],saleDate:['saleDate','Sale Date'],
      netRent:['netRent','Net Rent'],yld:['yld','Yield'],
      landArea:['landArea','Land Area (m2)'],landRate:['landRate','Land $ / m2'],
      floorArea:['floorArea','Floor Area (m2)'],wale:['wale','WALE'],
      agent:['agent','Agent'],coAgent:['coAgent','Co Agent'],
      vendor:['vendor','Vendor'],purchaser:['purchaser','Purchaser'],
      zoning:['zoning','Primary Zoning'],fsr:['fsr','FSR'],height:['height','Height (m)'],
      devStage:['devStage','Development Stage'],constraints:['constraints','Constraints'],
      comments:['comments','Comments'],configuration:['configuration','Configuration'],
      status:['status','Status'],process:['process','Process'],
      campaignClose:['campaignClose','Campaign Close Date'],
      settlementDate:['settlementDate','Settlement Date'],
      outgoings:['outgoings','Outgoings'],
    };
    const keys=aliases[key]||[key];
    for(const k of keys){const v=d[k];if(v!==null&&v!==undefined&&v!=='')return v;}
    return '';
  };
  Object.entries(m).forEach(([id,key])=>{const el=document.getElementById(id);if(!el)return;const v=get(key);if(v!==''&&v!==null&&v!==undefined)el.value=v;});
  const y=get('yld');if(y)document.getElementById('f_yield').value=parseFloat(y)<1?(parseFloat(y)*100).toFixed(2):y;
  const lr=get('landRate');if(lr)document.getElementById('f_landrate').value=Math.round(parseFloat(lr));
}
function getForm(){
  const g=id=>document.getElementById(id)?.value?.trim()||'';
  const n=id=>parseFloat(document.getElementById(id)?.value)||null;
  const yldVal=n('f_yield');
  return {
    address:g('f_address'),suburb:g('f_suburb'),region:g('f_region'),
    'Source Database':g('f_source')||'Metro',source:g('f_source')||'Metro',
    classification:g('f_class'),'Classification':g('f_class'),
    landArea:n('f_land'),floorArea:n('f_floor'),zoning:g('f_zoning'),
    fsr:n('f_fsr'),height:n('f_height'),configuration:g('f_config'),constraints:g('f_constraints'),
    devStage:g('f_devstage'),price:n('f_price'),'Sale Price':n('f_price'),
    priceGuide:n('f_priceguide'),saleDate:g('f_date'),'Sale Date':g('f_date'),
    netRent:n('f_netrent'),outgoings:n('f_outgoings'),
    campaignClose:g('f_closedate'),'Campaign Close Date':g('f_closedate'),
    settlementDate:g('f_settlementdate'),'Settlement Date':g('f_settlementdate'),
    wale:n('f_wale'),vendor:g('f_vendor'),purchaser:g('f_purchaser'),
    agent:g('f_agent'),'Agent':g('f_agent'),
    coAgent:g('f_coagent'),comments:g('f_comments'),
    status:g('f_status')||'Active','Status':g('f_status')||'Active',
    process:g('f_process'),'Process':g('f_process'),
    yld:yldVal?yldVal/100:null,'Yield':yldVal?yldVal/100:null,
    landRate:n('f_landrate'),'Land $ / m2':n('f_landrate'),
  };
}

async function saveSale(){
  const data=getForm();
  if(!data.address){toast('Address required');return;}
  const btn=document.getElementById('saveBtn');btn.disabled=true;btn.innerHTML='<span class="spin"></span> Saving…';
  if(data.price&&data.landArea&&!data.landRate){data.landRate=Math.round(data.price/data.landArea);data['Land $ / m2']=data.landRate;}
  if(data.price&&data.netRent&&!data.yld){data.yld=data.netRent/data.price;data['Yield']=data.yld;}
  // AI commentary
  try{data.analysis=await getAICommentary(data);}catch(e){}
  if(editId){
    const i=sales.findIndex(s=>(s.id||s['ID'])===editId);
    if(i>-1)sales[i]={...sales[i],...data};toast('Sale updated','ok');
  } else {
    data.id=gId();data.created=new Date().toISOString();
    sales.unshift(data);
    // Auto-add to campaign tracker if has close date
    if(data.campaignClose&&data.process){
      campaigns.unshift({id:gId(),address:data.address,suburb:data.suburb,classification:data.classification||'',process:data.process,closeDate:data.campaignClose,agent:data.agent||'',notes:data.comments?.substring(0,150)||'',status:'active',salePrice:null,purchaser:'',soldDate:'',settlementDate:'',rcStatus:''});
    }
    toast('Sale added','ok');
  }
  await syncDB();render();populateFilters();closeM('addModal');
  btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent='Save Sale';
}

function editSale(id){
  const s=sales.find(x=>(x.id||x['ID'])===id);if(!s)return;
  editId=id;document.getElementById('addTitle').textContent='Edit Sale';
  clearForm();fillForm(s);closeM('detailModal');openM('addModal');
}

async function getAICommentary(d){
  const r=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:1000,messages:[{role:'user',content:`You are a senior NSW commercial property analyst at Cushman & Wakefield. Write a concise 2-3 sentence investment commentary. Professional tone, factual, no fluff.
${d.address}, ${d.suburb} ${d.region} | ${d.classification||d['Classification']}
Price: ${d.price?'$'+Number(d.price).toLocaleString():'Undisclosed'} | Date: ${d.saleDate||'N/A'} | Yield: ${d.yld?(d.yld*100).toFixed(2)+'%':'N/A'} | Land Rate: ${d.landRate?'$'+Math.round(d.landRate)+'/m²':'N/A'}
Land: ${d.landArea||'N/A'}m² | GFA: ${d.floorArea||'N/A'}m² | Agent: ${d.agent||'N/A'} | Process: ${d.process||'N/A'}
${d.comments?'Notes: '+d.comments:''}

Respond with ONLY the commentary paragraph.`}]})});
  const j=await r.json();return j.content?.[0]?.text||null;
}

async function openDetail(id){
  const s=sales.find(x=>(x.id||x['ID'])===id);if(!s)return;
  const addr=getAddr(s),suburb=getSuburb(s);
  document.getElementById('detTitle').textContent=addr;
  document.getElementById('detSub').textContent=`${suburb}${getRegion(s)?' · '+getRegion(s):''} · ${getCls(s)||''} · ${fD(getSd(s))}`;

  const gmapsUrl=`https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(addr+' '+suburb+' NSW')}`;
  const svUrl=`https://www.google.com/maps/@?api=1&map_action=pano&query=${encodeURIComponent(addr+' '+suburb+' NSW')}`;

  const fields=[
    ['Sale Price',f$(getPrice(s)),true],['Net Income',s.netRent||s['Net Rent']?f$(s.netRent||s['Net Rent'])+'/yr':'—',false],
    ['Yield',yldD(getYld(s)),false],['Land Rate',fM(getLR(s)),false],
    ['Land Area',s.landArea||s['Land Area (m2)']?(s.landArea||s['Land Area (m2)'])+'m²':'—',false],
    ['Floor Area',s.floorArea||s['Floor Area (m2)']?(s.floorArea||s['Floor Area (m2)'])+'m²':'—',false],
    ['Zoning',s.zoning||s['Primary Zoning']||'—',false],['FSR',s.fsr||s['FSR']||'—',false],
    ['WALE',s.wale||s['WALE']?((s.wale||s['WALE'])+' yrs'):'—',false],
    ['Vendor',s.vendor||s['Vendor']||'—',false],['Purchaser',s.purchaser||s['Purchaser']||'—',false],
    ['Agent',getAgent(s)||'—',false],['Process',s.process||s['Process']||'—',false],
    ['Campaign Close',fD(getCC(s)),false],['Settlement Date',fD(s.settlementDate||s['Settlement Date']),false],
    ['RC Status',getRC(s)||'—',false],
    ['Status',`<span class="badge b-${getStatus(s).toLowerCase()}">${getStatus(s)}</span>`,false],
    ['Source',`<span class="db-tag ${srcCls(getSrc(s))}">${getSrc(s)}</span>`,false],
  ];

  const pg=getPG(s);

  document.getElementById('detBody').innerHTML=`
    <div class="prop-img-wrap" id="prop-img-wrap">
      <div class="prop-img-loading" id="prop-img-loading"><span class="spin"></span> Loading property view…</div>
      <img id="prop-img" src="" alt="" style="display:none;width:100%;height:200px;object-fit:cover">
      <div class="prop-img-links">
        <a href="${gmapsUrl}" target="_blank" class="prop-img-link red">📍 Maps</a>
        <a href="${svUrl}" target="_blank" class="prop-img-link">🚶 Street View</a>
      </div>
    </div>
    ${pg?`<div style="background:var(--amber-pale);border:1px solid rgba(184,134,11,.2);border-radius:2px;padding:.6rem .85rem;margin-bottom:.78rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem"><span style="font-size:.6rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--amber)">Price Guidance</span><span style="font-family:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;font-size:1.05rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--amber)">${f$(pg)}</span></div>`:''}
    <div class="dg">${fields.map(([l,v,big])=>`<div class="df"><div class="dl">${l}</div><div class="dv${big?' big':''}">${v}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>
    ${s.configuration||s['Configuration']?`<p style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.55rem">Config: ${s.configuration||s['Configuration']}</p>`:''}
    ${s.constraints||s['Constraints']?`<p style="font-size:.78rem;color:var(--mid);margin-bottom:.55rem">Constraints: ${s.constraints||s['Constraints']}</p>`:''}
    ${s.comments||s['Comments']?`<div class="abox"><div class="atitle">Notes</div><div class="atext">${s.comments||s['Comments']}</div></div>`:''}
    ${s.analysis?`<div class="abox" style="margin-top:.52rem"><div class="atitle">AI Investment Commentary</div><div class="atext">${s.analysis}</div></div>`:''}
    <div class="abox" style="margin-top:.52rem" id="notes-section-${id}"></div>`;

  renderNotes(id);
  document.getElementById('detEdit').onclick=()=>editSale(id);
  document.getElementById('detDel').onclick=()=>deleteSale(id);
  openM('detailModal');

  // Fetch property image async
  fetchPropertyImage(addr,suburb);
}

async function fetchPropertyImage(address,suburb){
  try{
    const resp=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:500,tools:[{type:'web_search_20250305',name:'web_search'}],messages:[{role:'user',content:`Search for a street-level photo or property image of "${address}, ${suburb} NSW Australia". Return ONLY JSON: {"imageUrl":"direct_image_url_or_null","description":"one_sentence_property_description_or_null"}. Use null if no clear image found. JSON only, no markdown.`}]})});
    const d=await resp.json();
    const txt=d.content?.filter(b=>b.type==='text').map(b=>b.text).join('')||'{}';
    const result=JSON.parse(txt.replace(/```json|```/g,'').trim());
    const loading=document.getElementById('prop-img-loading');
    const img=document.getElementById('prop-img');
    if(loading)loading.style.display='none';
    if(result.imageUrl&&img){
      const test=new Image();
      test.onload=()=>{img.src=result.imageUrl;img.style.display='block';};
      test.onerror=()=>{};
      test.src=result.imageUrl;
    }
  }catch(e){
    const loading=document.getElementById('prop-img-loading');
    if(loading)loading.style.display='none';
  }
}

async function deleteSale(id){if(!confirm('Delete?'))return;sales=sales.filter(s=>(s.id||s['ID'])!==id);await syncDB();render();closeM('detailModal');toast('Deleted');}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// CAMPAIGN MODAL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function addCampaignModal(){
  ['ca_addr','ca_suburb','ca_agent','ca_notes'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).value='');
  ['ca_class'].forEach(id=>document.getElementById(id).value='');
  document.getElementById('ca_process').value='Auction';
  document.getElementById('ca_date').value=today();
  openM('campModal');
}
async function saveCampaign(){
  const addr=document.getElementById('ca_addr').value.trim();
  const date=document.getElementById('ca_date').value;
  if(!addr||!date){toast('Address and date required');return;}
  campaigns.unshift({id:gId(),address:addr,suburb:document.getElementById('ca_suburb').value.trim(),classification:document.getElementById('ca_class').value,process:document.getElementById('ca_process').value,closeDate:date,agent:document.getElementById('ca_agent').value.trim(),notes:document.getElementById('ca_notes').value.trim(),status:'active',salePrice:null,purchaser:'',soldDate:'',settlementDate:'',rcStatus:''});
  await syncDB();render();closeM('campModal');toast('Campaign added','ok');
}
async function deleteCamp(id){if(!confirm('Remove campaign?'))return;campaigns=campaigns.filter(c=>c.id!==id);await syncDB();renderTracker();updateStats();updateBadges();}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// PDF SINGLE EXTRACT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function openPDFModal(){
  document.getElementById('pdfStatus').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('pdfPreview').innerHTML='';
  document.getElementById('pdfUseBtn').style.display='none';pdfData=null;openM('pdfModal');
}
const pdfDrop=document.getElementById('pdfDrop');
pdfDrop.addEventListener('dragover',e=>{e.preventDefault();pdfDrop.classList.add('over');});
pdfDrop.addEventListener('dragleave',()=>pdfDrop.classList.remove('over'));
pdfDrop.addEventListener('drop',e=>{e.preventDefault();pdfDrop.classList.remove('over');const f=e.dataTransfer.files[0];if(f?.type==='application/pdf')processPDF(f);});
function handlePDF(e){const f=e.target.files[0];if(f)processPDF(f);}
async function processPDF(file){
  const st=document.getElementById('pdfStatus');
  st.innerHTML='<div style="background:var(--red-mid);color:var(--red);padding:.42rem .75rem;border-radius:2px;font-size:.77rem;margin-top:.52rem"><span class="spin"></span> Extracting from PDF…</div>';
  document.getElementById('pdfPreview').innerHTML='';document.getElementById('pdfUseBtn').style.display='none';
  try{
    const b64=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{const r=new FileReader();r.onload=()=>res(r.result.split(',')[1]);r.onerror=rej;r.readAsDataURL(file);});
    const resp=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',max_tokens:1000,messages:[{role:'user',content:[{type:'document',source:{type:'base64',media_type:'application/pdf',data:b64}},{type:'text',text:'Extract all commercial property details from this document. Search every page including page 4+ for net income/rent figures. Return ONLY valid JSON: {"address":null,"suburb":null,"classification":null,"zoning":null,"landArea":null,"floorArea":null,"fsr":null,"height":null,"price":null,"saleDate":null,"campaignClose":null,"netRent":null,"outgoings":null,"yld":null,"wale":null,"vendor":null,"purchaser":null,"agent":null,"coAgent":null,"process":null,"status":"Active","configuration":null,"constraints":null,"devStage":null,"comments":null,"source":"Metro"}'}]}]})});
    const data=await resp.json();
    pdfData=JSON.parse((data.content?.[0]?.text||'{}').replace(/```json|```/g,'').trim());
    if(pdfData.price&&pdfData.landArea)pdfData.landRate=Math.round(pdfData.price/pdfData.landArea);
    if(pdfData.price&&pdfData.netRent)pdfData.yld=pdfData.netRent/pdfData.price;
    st.innerHTML='<div style="background:var(--green-pale);color:var(--green);padding:.42rem .75rem;border-radius:2px;font-size:.77rem;margin-top:.52rem">✓ Extracted successfully</div>';
    const rows=[['Address',pdfData.address],['Suburb',pdfData.suburb],['Type',pdfData.classification],['Price',pdfData.price?f$(pdfData.price):null],['Net Income',pdfData.netRent?f$(pdfData.netRent)+'/yr':null],['Yield',pdfData.yld?(pdfData.yld*100).toFixed(2)+'%':null],['Land',pdfData.landArea?pdfData.landArea+'m²':null],['Agent',pdfData.agent],['Close Date',fD(pdfData.campaignClose)]].filter(([,v])=>v&&v!=='—');
    document.getElementById('pdfPreview').innerHTML=`<div style="margin-top:.65rem;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:2px;overflow:hidden">${rows.map(([l,v],i)=>`<div style="display:flex;padding:.38rem .65rem;${i%2===0?'background:var(--pale)':''}"><div style="font-size:.58rem;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--mid);min-width:95px;padding-top:.1rem">${l}</div><div style="font-size:.8rem;font-weight:500">${v}</div></div>`).join('')}</div>`;
    document.getElementById('pdfUseBtn').style.display='inline-flex';
  }catch(e){st.innerHTML='<div style="background:var(--red-mid);color:var(--red);padding:.42rem .75rem;border-radius:2px;font-size:.77rem;margin-top:.52rem">✕ Could not extract — try again</div>';}
}
function usePDF(){if(!pdfData)return;closeM('pdfModal');openAddModal();fillForm({...pdfData,landRate:pdfData.landRate||null});toast('PDF data loaded — review and save');}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// SYNC — single function for all writes
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function syncDB(){
  if(!gsReady)return;
  try{await gsPushAllSales();await gsPushAllCamps();}
  catch(e){console.warn('Sync failed:',e.message);}
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// INIT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// Render seed data when DOM is ready
async function load(){
  try { render(); } catch(e) { console.warn('render:', e); }
  try { populateFilters(); } catch(e) { console.warn('filters:', e); }
  try { populateBenchmarkSuburbs(); } catch(e) {}
  try { runDupCheck(true); } catch(e) {}
  try { await gsLoad(); } catch(e) { console.warn('gsLoad:', e); }
}
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', load);
</script>
</body>
</html>
